\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}

(92)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\mathbf{\ell}}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{e^{i\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x})}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}[U_{\mathbf{z}}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}}t^{c}-C_{F}]

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}}{2}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

In the transversely polarized case, the amplitude is given by:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V3}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{s}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{3}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\bar{\lambda}\eta^{\prime}}

(93)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}[U_{\mathbf{z}}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}}t^{c}-C_{F}]\;.

We can show that, although we cannot analytically
evaluate the above transverse integration, it is free from UV divergences.

Diagram V4

One obtains for the vertex correction where the outgoing quark scatters
off the shockwave
3
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(94)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[-\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)\Bigg]\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})

\displaystyle-2\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{(k^{+}q^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}

(95)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{2}+p_{0}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Bigg]\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})

\displaystyle+\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{2}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)-\frac{k^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})M^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D-3}\frac{1}{2}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Bigg]\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The UV-counterterms read:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(96)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)\Bigg]\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{(k^{+}q^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}

(97)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{2}+p_{0}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Bigg]\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Therefore, the subtracted amplitudes become:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(98)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[-\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)\Bigg]\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle-2\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{(k^{+}q^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}

(99)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{2}+p_{0}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Bigg]\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle+\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{1}{2}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)+\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})\mathbf{q}^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D-3}\frac{1}{2}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Bigg]\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

3.3
Antiquark vertex corrections

Figure 4:
The four virtual contributions with a virtual gluon
and a virtual antiquark.

Diagram A1

In the case of longitudinal polarization, one obtains for the amplitude
corresponding to diagram A1 in fig.
4
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(100)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[2M^{2}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\hat{M}^{2})+\frac{(q^{+})^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})

\displaystyle-2M^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}M^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Note that
\hat{M}^{2}
is defined in (
7
). In the transverse case, we find:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(101)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\hat{M}^{2})\Big(\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Big)

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Big)\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{2p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})-k^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big((p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}\big)\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})M^{2}\Big)\Bigg)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle-\!\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\!\Big(\!\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\!-\!\hat{M}^{2}\!-\!\Big(\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\!\Big)^{\!2}\Big)\!\frac{1}{D\!-\!3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\!\hat{M}^{2},\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\!\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\!\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Note that the spinor structures appearing here are the same as the
‘vertex’ ones (
77
) and (
74
).
Moreover, the amplitudes are all divergent in the ultraviolet, and
the singularities are contained in the structures
\mathcal{A}_{0}
.
We can extract it by defining the counterterms:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(102)

\displaystyle\times\Big(2M^{2}+\frac{(q^{+})^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}q^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(103)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The subtracted amplitudes read:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\!\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\!\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\!\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\Big(\!\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-1\!\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\big)\!+\!1\!\Big)

(104)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(2M^{2}\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\hat{M}^{2}}+\frac{(q^{+})^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle-2M^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}M^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg]\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{\lambda}=\frac{\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(105)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\Big(\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Big)\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\Big)\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{2p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})-k^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big((p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}\big)\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})M^{2}\Big)\Bigg)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle-\!\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\!\Big(\!\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\!-\!\hat{M}^{2}\!-\!\Big(\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\!\Big)^{\!2}\Big)\!\frac{1}{D\!-\!3}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\!\hat{M}^{2},\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\!\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\!\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Diagram A2

For the production amplitudes of a longitudinally or transversely
polarized virtual photon, we obtain, respectively:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}

(106)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\frac{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}-\hat{M}^{2}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}+\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{s}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\bar{\lambda}\eta^{\prime}}

(107)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\frac{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}\Big)}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}+\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)\;.

Similarly to the amplitudes
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V2,3}}
,
the transverse integrals over
\boldsymbol{\ell}
do not admit an
analytic solution, at least to the best of our knowledge. It is, however,
possible to study their behavior in the limit
\lim_{\mathbf{z}\to\mathbf{x}}

where they might exhibit a UV divergence, which turns out to be the
case for the ‘longitudinal’ amplitude
106
:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathbf{z}\to\mathbf{x}}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(108)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})-2\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle-2\hat{M}^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\mathcal{C}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The counterterm, therefore, reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(109)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The subtracted amplitude becomes:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}

(110)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\frac{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}-\hat{M}^{2}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}+\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}}{D-2}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

Amplitude
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{\lambda}
turns out
to be UV-finite.

Diagram A3

We obtain for the amplitudes where both the virtual quark and antiquark,
as well as the outgoing quark scatter off the shockwave (fig.
4
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{2k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}

(111)

\displaystyle\times\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}-\mathbf{x}_{2},\hat{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}\Big)}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{c}-C_{F}]\;,

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{s}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\bar{\lambda}\eta^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}

(112)

\displaystyle\times iA^{\bar{\lambda}}\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}-\mathbf{x}_{2},\hat{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}\Big)}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{c}-C_{F}]\;.

In the above expressions, we used the short-hand notation
\mathbf{x}_{ij}=\mathbf{x}_{i}-\mathbf{x}_{j}
.
Investigating for possible UV divergences in the limit
\mathbf{x}_{1}\to\mathbf{x}_{2}\to\mathbf{x}_{3}
,
it turns out that
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{\lambda}
is
finite while
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{0}
contains an
ultraviolet pole, which can be absorbed into the counterterm:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(113)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The subtracted amplitude becomes:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}

(114)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}2\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}-\mathbf{x}_{2},\hat{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}\Big)}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{c}-C_{F}]

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}}{D-2}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

Diagram A4

In the longitudinal case, the amplitude is given by:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(115)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\mathcal{A}_{0}(\hat{Q}^{2})+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

where we have defined:

\hat{Q}^{2}\equiv-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\mathbf{q}^{2}+i0^{+}\;.

(116)

Likewise, in the transverse case, we find:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{2}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(117)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D-3}\Bigg]\mathcal{A}_{0}(\hat{Q}^{2})

\displaystyle+\Bigg[\!-\!\Big(\hat{Q}^{2}\!-\!\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\Big)^{2}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D\!-\!3}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Clearly, both
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4}}^{0}
and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4}}^{\lambda}

are divergent in the ultraviolet. The poles are extracted by defining
the counterterms:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\frac{1}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}

(118)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{2}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(119)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D-3}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

The subtracted amplitudes, therefore, become:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{4k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)

(120)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\hat{Q}^{2}}+4\pi\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Big)\;,

in the longitudinally polarized case, and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{q}^{\rho}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{2}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(121)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Bigg[\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}+\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D-3}\Bigg]\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\hat{Q}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\!\Bigg[\!-\!\Big(\hat{Q}^{2}\!-\!\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\Big)^{2}\Big)\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\rho j}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\bar{\lambda}j}\epsilon^{\rho\bar{\lambda}}\frac{1}{D\!-\!3}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

when the photon is transversally polarized.

3.4
Instantaneous four-fermion interaction

Figure 5:
The three virtual contributions with an instantaneous

qq\bar{q}q
vertex or, equivalently, a fictitious instantaneous
gluon in the
t
-channel.

Diagram Q1

We obtain the following amplitudes for the first diagram in figure

5
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\!\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}M}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{8p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\tilde{M}^{2})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big)\;,

(122)

with:

\tilde{M}^{2}\equiv-\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}-i0^{+}\;.

(123)

In the transverse case, the amplitude simply disappears (it is proportional
to an integral of the form
\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{i}/(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta)
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=0\;.

(124)

The UV counterterm for
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1}}^{0}
is
given by:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}M}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\!\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{8p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}\big)\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)

(125)

such that the subtracted amplitude is equal to:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1\!,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}M}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\!\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{8p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{1}{4\pi}\!\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\tilde{M}^{2}}\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big).

(126)

Diagram Q2

We obtain for the amplitude in which the outgoing photon is longitudinally
polarized:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{8\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{1}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+}}

(127)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}\tilde{M}^{2}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}_{12},\tilde{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{13}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{\ell_{1}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})(p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}\Big)}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{a}-C_{F}]\;,

and for the transverse case:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=-4\alpha_{s}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{1}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\Big(\frac{2\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-1\Big)

(128)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}iA^{\bar{\lambda}}\big(\mathbf{x}_{12},\tilde{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{13}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{\ell_{1}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})(p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}\Big)}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{a}-C_{F}]\;.

To investigate whether these amplitudes contain any divergences, we
will take the
\mathbf{x}_{3}\to\mathbf{x}_{2}\to\mathbf{x}_{1}\to\mathbf{x}

limit in the non-divergent piece, i.e. the last line, which allows
us to evaluate the integrals over
\mathbf{x}_{2}
and
\mathbf{x}_{3}

in the transverse momentum integrations. We easily obtain:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathbf{x}_{i}\to\mathbf{x}}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\tilde{M}^{2})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)

(129)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{finite}\;\mathrm{terms}\;,

where we made us of identities and (
406
)
407
.
The counterterm for
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2,UV}}^{0}
, therefore,
reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

(130)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\Big(-2+\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and the subtracted amplitude is given by:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{8\alpha_{s}}{M}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{1}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}\tilde{M}^{2}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}_{12},\tilde{M}^{2}\big)

(131)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{13}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{\ell_{1}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})(p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}\Big)}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}[U_{\mathbf{x}_{3}}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}_{2}}^{\dagger}U_{\mathbf{x}_{1}}t^{a}-C_{F}]

\displaystyle-\Big(1-\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{2q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

In the transversely polarized case, the amplitude turns out to be
free from UV divergences:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathbf{x}_{i}\to\mathbf{x}}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=0\;.

(132)

Diagram Q3

We obtain the following expressions for the production amplitudes
for a longitudinally resp. transversally polarized virtual photon:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\!\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{4\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\tilde{Q}^{2})\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big)\;,

(133)

with

\tilde{Q}^{2}\equiv\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\mathbf{q}^{2}-i0^{+}\;.

(134)

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=0\;.

(135)

The counterterm for
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3}}^{0}
is given
by:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!-\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\!\!\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{4\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}\!e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(\!U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\!\big)\!\,,

(136)

such that the subtracted amplitude reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!-\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{4\pi M}\!\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\!\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{4\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\tilde{Q}^{2}}\!\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}\!e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big)\,.

3.5
Instantaneous
gq\gamma q
interaction

Figure 6:
The four virtual diagrams with an instantaneous
qg\gamma q
-vertex.
Two additional diagrams where the gluon is attached to the asymptotic
incoming or outgoing quark disappear.

Diagram I1

We obtain the following result for the amplitude corresponding to
graph I1 (see figure
6
) in the longitudinal case:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\!\!\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\!\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{\!2}\!\frac{q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!+\!D\!-\!4\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})\!\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}\!e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big)\!\,.

(138)

The amplitude disappears when the outgoing virtual photon is transversely
polarized:

\displaystyle\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{I1}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=0\;.

(139)

Clearly,
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1}}^{0}
has a ultraviolet
divergence, contained inside
\mathcal{A}_{0}
. We extract it by
defining the counterterm:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\Big)

(140)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

such that the subtracted amplitude reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{4\pi M}\frac{q^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}}\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

(141)

Diagram I2

We obtain for the amplitude when the virtual photon is longitudinally
polarized:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\eta^{\prime}}\big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)

(142)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{q^{+}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})}{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}(\delta^{\eta i}+i\sigma^{\eta i})

\displaystyle-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)\Big(\delta^{\eta i}+\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}i\sigma^{\eta i}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)\;.

Likewise, in the transverse case:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{s}\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)

(143)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\eta\lambda}+\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)i\sigma^{\eta\lambda}\Bigg]\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\Big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)\;.

Not surprisingly, the amplitude
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{0}

contains an ultraviolet divergence in the limit
\mathbf{z}\to\mathbf{x}
:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathbf{z}\to\mathbf{x}}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\big)

(144)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Defining the counterterm:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\Big)

(145)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1,UV}}^{0}\;,

we obtain the following result for the UV-subtracted amplitude:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\eta^{\prime}}\big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)

(146)

\displaystyle\times\!\!\Bigg(\!\!\frac{q^{+}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\!\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})}{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}(\delta^{\eta i}\!+\!i\sigma^{\eta i})\!-\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)\!\Big(\!\delta^{\eta i}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}i\sigma^{\eta i}\!\Big)\!\!\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\!e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\big(t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\!\frac{2q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

Diagram I3

The results for the amplitudes corresponding to the Feynman graph

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I3}}^{0}

in the limit

(149)

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{k^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\big)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

such that the subtracted amplitude becomes:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{M}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})

(151)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}

\displaystyle+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{i}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}}\big(1-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)-1\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(t^{a}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{a}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

Diagram I4

We obtain for the last diagram in figure
6
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I4}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{q^{+}k^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\big)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I4}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=0\;.

(153)

The counterterm reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{M}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{q^{+}k^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}+D-4\big)

(154)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I3,UV}}^{0}\;,

with the subtracted amplitude:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{2\pi M}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(k^{+}-p_{0}^{+})}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

(155)

3.6
Field-strength renormalization

Figure 7:
The four virtual contributions with a gluon loop on
the asymptotic in- or outgoing quark.

The last class of virtual diagrams that we consider are those corresponding
to self-energy corrections on the asymptotic quarks. Although the
corresponding amplitudes vanish in dimensional renormalization, we
will compute them explicitly. Indeed, they consist of two identical
parts with an opposite sign, one corresponding to an UV pole, the
other to an IR one. Since in dimensional renormalization
\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}=\epsilon_{\mathrm{IR}}
,
the net result is zero, although in our analysis we will treat the
ultraviolet and infrared parts separately.

It should be noted that, in LCPT, it is not possible to compute these
amplitudes with our usual method, since they feature an energy denominator

\propto(p_{0}^{-}-p_{0}^{-})^{-1}
. Instead, we make use of the
normalization properties of the Fock states. As demonstrated in appendix
(
E
), the total contribution of the diagrams in fig.

7
can be summarized into the following field-renormalization
constant:

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}

\displaystyle=\Bigg[1-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]\;,

(156)

which appears on the level of the squared amplitude as:

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\dagger}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Z1}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Z2}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Z3}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Z4}}\big)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{Z}-1)\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\;,

(157)

irrespective of the polarization of the emitted virtual photon.

In anticipation of what follows, we can rewrite
\mathcal{Z}
as

\mathcal{Z}=1+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\dagger}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}^{\dagger}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{FS}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{FS}}^{\dagger}\;,

(158)

with:

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(\frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;,

(159)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{8}+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;,

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{FS}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{8}+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;.

Since the scale
\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}
cancels on the cross-section level,
we are free to adjust it at will.

4
Ultraviolet divergences

At the perturbative order of our calculation, all short-distance or
ultraviolet divergences are contained in the virtual corrections presented
in the previous section. Because contributions to the running coupling
take place at higher perturbative orders, and since we work in the
massless limit, these singularities have to cancel on the level of
the total virtual amplitude. In this section, we will demonstrate
that this is indeed the case, which is an important check of the calculation.

Two classes of virtual amplitudes, namely the self-energy corrections
in section
3.1
, and the ones with an instantaneous
gq\gamma q

interaction (section
3.5
), are somewhat particular.
Indeed, we have seen that (see eqs. (
60
), (
61
),
and (
71
)):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE2,UV}}^{0,\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,UV}}^{0,\lambda}\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,UV}}^{0,\lambda}=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE3,UV}}^{0,\lambda}\;,

(160)

and, therefore, all UV divergences already cancel in the sum of amplitudes
belonging to the same class. The same is true for the amplitudes with
an instantaneous
gq\gamma q
vertex and a longitudinally polarized
photon (eqs. (
145
) and (
154
)):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I1,UV}}^{0}\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I4,UV}}^{0}=-\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{I3,UV}}^{0}\;,

(161)

while in the transverse case, the amplitudes never exhibit UV divergencies
to begin with.

All the ultraviolet divergences we encountered are contained in integrals

\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta)
(
406
) which, when evaluated in
dimensional regularization, give:

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta)

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta}=\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln 4\pi+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta}\Big)+\mathcal{O}(\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

(162)

Following the
\overline{\mathrm{MS}}
scheme, we will extract the
poles together with the universal constants
-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln 4\pi
,
writing:

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta}\Big)+\mathcal{O}(\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}})\;,

(163)

where we defined:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln 4\pi\;.

(164)

4.1
Longitudinal polarization

Vertex corrections

We start by collecting the UV-divergent parts of the vertex-correction
amplitudes, which are collected by the counterterms. The counterterms
(
78
) and (
85
) can be combined into:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;,

(165)

while summing the poles from (
91
), and (
94
)
gives:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V3,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

(166)

In the above expressions,
\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}
is the symmetric part of the Dirac structure (
74
). We immediately find:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{0}\!+\!\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\!\Big(\!\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}\!+\!\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\!\Big(\!-\!\frac{3p_{1}^{+}\!+\!2q^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\!+\!\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\!-\!\frac{1}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg]\;,

(167)

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V3,UV}}^{0}\!+\!\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\!\Big(\!\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}\!+\!\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\!\Big(\!-\!\frac{3p_{1}^{+}\!+\!2q^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\!+\!\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\!-\!\frac{1}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg]\;.

(168)

Antiquark vertex corrections

Likewise, let us collect the UV counterterms for the four amplitudes
with an antiquark vertex. We see that the first three of them:
102
),
(
109
), and (
113
), nicely combine into:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A2,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A3,UV}}^{0}

(169)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

The UV-counterterm (
118
) is equal to:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j0j}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

(170)

Using expression (
74
) for the symmetrized part of the
Dirac structure, the plus-momentum integral in the above expressions
can be easily evaluated, yielding:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1+2+3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}\!+\!\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(\frac{7}{4}\!+\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\!-\!\frac{3p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{0}^{+}}{2q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\!-\!\frac{q^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg]\;,

(171)

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(\frac{7}{4}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{3p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{2q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)-\frac{q^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg]\;.

(172)

Instantaneous four-fermion vertex

Another category of ultraviolet-singular virtual diagrams are those
with an instantaneous four-fermion vertex. Collecting the counterterms
(
125
) and (
130
), we obtain an expression
proportional to the leading-order amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}

(
32
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}4\alpha_{s}C_{F}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

(173)

Likewise, the counterterm (
136
) for the amplitude
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3}}^{0}

is:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}4\alpha_{s}C_{F}\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\;.

(174)

Evaluating the plus-momentum integrals, we obtain:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1+2,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(-2+\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\;,

(175)

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3,UV}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(-2+\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\;.

(176)

Total UV contribution

The last set of UV divergences are those stemming from the field-strength
renormalization diagrams (eqs. (
157
), (
159
)):

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1,2}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\!\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1,2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}\!-\!\ln 4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\!+\!\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(\frac{3}{4}\!-\!\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\!-\!\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;.

(177)

Combining them with the results from the previous paragraphs, namely
(
167
), (
171
), and (
175
),
we obtain:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1+2,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1+2+3,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q1+2,UV}}^{0}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}

(178)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\frac{1}{4}\Bigg]\;.

Likewise, combining (
177
) with eqs. (
168
),
(
172
), and (
176
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V3+4,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{0}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{Q3,UV}}^{0}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}

(179)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\frac{1}{4}\Bigg]\;.

The above two results confirm our earlier claim, namely that in the
longitudinal case all the UV divergences encountered in our calculation
cancel between the different virtual diagrams. Note that there are
still divergences left, in the form of single logarithms depending
on the plus-momentum cutoff
k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
. In section
8
,
we will show how they can be absorbed into the high-energy evolution
of the target gluon density.

4.2
Transverse polarization

When the outgoing virtual photon is transversely polarized, the structure
of the UV cancellation is slightly different compared to the previous
subsection. In particular, there are no contributions from the diagrams
with an instantaneous four-fermion vertex. Moreover, since the Dirac
structure is now much more complicated than in the longitudinal case,
we are forced to replace the spinor structures with simpler ones,
obviously in such a way that this procedure still yields the correct
cross section. Hence, although the cancellation of UV divergences
should already take place at the level of the amplitude, as was the
case in the previous section, in practice we will demonstrate this
cancellation in a way closer to the level of the squared amplitude.

The first step is to expand the ‘even’ and ‘odd’
parts of the Dirac structure
\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\bar{\lambda}\eta^{\prime}}

(
77
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\bar{\lambda}j}

\displaystyle=(D-2)\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)\Big)\delta^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}

(180)

\displaystyle-(D-2)\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)+8\frac{D-4}{D-2}\Big)i\sigma^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\rho\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\Bigg[\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-2-(D-4)\Big)\delta^{\lambda\rho}i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}

(181)

\displaystyle-\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}+\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}+i\sigma^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\eta\eta^{\prime}}\Bigg]\;.

All the amplitudes we consider here are virtual, hence they will contribute
to the cross section being multiplied with the conjugate of the LO
amplitude, which contains the Dirac structure:

\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{\dagger}=\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\delta^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}+i\sigma^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}\;.

(182)

After multiplication with the above Dirac structure and evaluating
the trace, the different spinor structures appearing in (
180
)
and (
181
) all become proportional to
\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\delta^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=2\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\;,

(183)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)i\sigma^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=-2(D-3)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\delta^{\lambda\rho}i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=4\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=4\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-(D-4)\Big)\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\Big\}

\displaystyle=4\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}+D-4\Big)\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Tr}\Big\{\mathcal{P}_{G}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}\dagger}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)i\sigma^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\eta\eta^{\prime}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=4\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}(D-4)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\;,

where we made use of the identities in appendix
B
.
Therefore, in anticipation of the fact that the virtual amplitudes
we consider will be multiplied with the leading-order one, we can
rescale the Dirac structures in (
180
) and (
181
)
as follows:

\displaystyle i\sigma^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\to-(D-3)\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;,

(184)

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\lambda\rho}i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\to 2\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\to 2\Big(1-(D-4)\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}

\displaystyle\to 2\Big(1+(D-4)\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;,

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}i\sigma^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\sigma^{\lambda\rho}\sigma^{\eta\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\to 2(D-4)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;,

such that the even and odd parts of
\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}

can be replaced by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\bar{\lambda}j}

\displaystyle\to(D-2)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\Big(\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+(D-3)\Big)

(185)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)\Big)+8\frac{(D-4)(D-3)}{D-2}\Bigg]\;,

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\rho\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\to\frac{8q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\Bigg[\Big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\Big)

(186)

\displaystyle+\frac{D-4}{2}\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{k^{+}}+\frac{k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]\;.

Likewise, the leading-order Dirac structure can be replaced by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)

\displaystyle\to\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+(D-3)\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda}\;.

(187)

In the following paragraphs, we will use an underline to denote the
(reduced) amplitudes in which the rescaling procedure (
184
)
has been performed. For instance, the leading-order amplitudes (
29
)
become:

\displaystyle\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{-q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big[U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big]\;,

(188)

\displaystyle\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{-q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big[U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big]\;.

Vertex corrections

Let us first study the part of (
79
) that contains the
UV divergence. Applying the rescaling procedure
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}\to\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}

and expanding
\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}})
(
52
), we
get:

\displaystyle\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}\!+\!D\!-\!3}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{2}}{4p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}

(189)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\Big(\frac{1}{2}+p_{0}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\Big)\Bigg[\Big(\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+(D-3)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)\Big)+8\frac{(D-4)(D-3)}{D-2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{(D\!-\!3)(D\!-\!2)}\Bigg[\!\Big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\Big)\!+\!\frac{D-4}{2}\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{k^{+}}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\!\Bigg]\!\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)

Expanding around
\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}=0
, we obtain, introducing
for simplicity
x=k^{+}/p_{1}^{+}
,
a=p_{0}^{+}/p_{1}^{+}
, and

b=k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}/p_{1}^{+}
:

\displaystyle\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\frac{3}{4}\frac{a(a-1)}{a^{2}+1}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x-1}

(190)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{1+2a}{2(1+a^{2})}-\ln b\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{6a^{5}-17a^{4}+14a^{3}-6a^{2}-8a+3}{8a(a^{2}+1)^{2}}-\frac{3}{4}\frac{a-1}{a^{2}+1}\ln\frac{a-1}{a}\Bigg\}\;.

Following the same procedure for (
95
), we get:

\displaystyle\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\frac{3}{4}\frac{a(a\!-\!1)}{a^{2}\!+\!1}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x\!-\!1}\!+\!\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}\!+\!\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\!\Big(\!-\frac{1\!+\!2a}{2(1\!+\!a^{2})}\!-\!\ln b\Big)

(191)

\displaystyle+\frac{6a^{5}-17a^{4}+14a^{3}-6a^{2}-8a+3}{8a(a^{2}+1)^{2}}-\frac{3}{4}\frac{a-1}{a^{2}+1}\ln\frac{a-1}{a}\Bigg\}\;.

Antiquark vertex diagrams

The counterterm
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}

(
103
) reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{D-2}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(192)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{j\bar{\lambda}j}+\frac{1}{D-3}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\rho}\epsilon^{ij}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{i\rho j}\Bigg\}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

or, after rescaling in the sense of eq. (
184
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{8p_{1}^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(193)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Bigg\{\Bigg[\Big(\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+(D-3)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)\Big)+8\frac{(D-4)(D-3)}{D-2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{(D\!-\!3)(D\!-\!2)}\Bigg[\Big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\Big)\!+\!\frac{D-4}{2}\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{k^{+}}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\!\Bigg]\!\Bigg\}\;.

Expanding around
\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}=0
, and reintroducing
x=k^{+}/p_{1}^{+}
,

a=p_{0}^{+}/p_{1}^{+}
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1+2a}{2(1+a^{2})}+\frac{1}{2}\ln a\Big)

(194)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\frac{a(a-1)}{a^{2}+1}\int_{1}^{a}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x-1}+(a-1)\frac{3+6a-18a^{2}+18a^{3}-17a^{4}}{8a(a^{2}+1)^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Likewise, the UV divergent part of (
117
) becomes:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\times-\alpha_{s}C_{F}\frac{1}{\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

(195)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Bigg\{\Bigg[\Big(\big(1-2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+(D-3)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)-(D-3)\Big)+8\frac{(D-4)(D-3)}{D-2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{(D\!-\!2)(D\!-\!3)}\Bigg[\!\Big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\Big)\!+\!\frac{D\!-\!4}{2}\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{k^{+}}\!+\!\frac{k^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\!\Bigg]\!\Bigg\}\;.

Or, after performing the integrations over the gluon plus-momentum:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\Big(\frac{1}{\tilde{\epsilon}}+\ln\frac{\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1+2a}{2(1+a^{2})}+\frac{1}{2}\ln a\Big)

(196)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\frac{a(a-1)}{a^{2}+1}\int_{1}^{a}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x-1}+(a-1)\frac{3+6a-18a^{2}+18a^{3}-17a^{4}}{8a(a^{2}+1)^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Total UV contributions

In order to add the results (
190
) and (
194
),
some closer attention is needed to the two integrals we left unevaluated.
Indeed, there is a singularity for
x\to 1
at the end- resp. starting
point of the leftover integration in
\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}

and
\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}
.
Introducing an infinitesimal regulator
i0^{+}
,
3
3

3

Note that, whenever necessary, we consistently assign a positive infinitesimal
imaginary part to the gluon plus-momentum.
these integrals can be added and evaluated using the Sokhotski–Plemelj
theorem:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x-1+i0^{+}}+\int_{1}^{a}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x-1+i0^{+}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-1}^{a-1}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{y+i0^{+}}=\mathcal{P}\int_{-1}^{a-1}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{y}-i\pi\;,

(197)

\displaystyle=\ln(a-1)-i\pi\;.

The same holds for the integrals in (
191
) and (
196
).

With the above manipulation, we are finally in a position to add (
190
)
and (
194
) to the contribution from the quark
field-strength renormalization (
159
), yielding:

\displaystyle\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V1,UV}}^{\lambda}+\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A1,UV}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}

(198)

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\frac{3}{4}\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big(\frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{11}{8}+\frac{9}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}-\frac{3}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg\}\;,

and similarly for (
191
) and (
196
):

\displaystyle\underline{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}_{\mathrm{V4,UV}}^{\lambda}+\tilde{\mathcal{\underline{M}}}_{\mathrm{A4,UV}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{UV}}\mathcal{\tilde{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}

(199)

\displaystyle=\underline{\mathcal{\tilde{M}}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\frac{3}{4}\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big(\frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{11}{8}+\frac{9}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}-\frac{3}{4}\frac{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg\}\;.

Hence, we have proven that, also in the case of a transversely polarized
photon, the total virtual contribution to the cross section is free
from UV divergences. Just like in the longitudinal case, however,
there are rapidity divergences left, which will be treated in section

8
.

Note that, in this section, all our expressions are proportional to
one of the two leading-order amplitudes. We have chosen to keep these
amplitudes defined in
D
dimensions. However, we have checked that,
when factorizing the LO amplitudes out in
D=4
dimensions instead,
the extra finite terms that appear all compensate each other. One,
therefore, obtains exactly the same result irrespective of the precise
procedure followed, which is an interesting nontrivial check of our
calculation.

4.3
Contribution to the cross section due to UV counterterms

Collecting our results (
178
