) and (
179
)
for the sum of the UV counterterms, and applying the definition (
6
),
we eventually obtain the following contribution to the cross section
in case of a longitudinally polarized photon:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\mathrm{L}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{L}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg]\;.

(200)

Similarly, (
198
) and (
199
) can
be combined into:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\mathrm{T}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{T}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}

(201)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+3\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)-\frac{11}{4}\Bigg]\;.

5
Real next-to-leading order corrections

In this section, we present the amplitudes for the real NLO corrections.
One should be very careful to note that, due to momentum conservation,
the plus-momentum of the incoming quark is equal to

p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}=p_{1}^{+}+q^{+}+p_{3}^{+}\;.

(202)

We denote this quantity with an additional index
R
, to avoid confusion
with the same momentum component but in leading-order and virtual
amplitudes, where
p_{0}^{+}=p_{1}^{+}+q^{+}
.

5.1
Initial-state radiation

Figure 8:
The four diagrams corresponding to real gluon emission
in the initial state.

Diagram IS1

The reduced amplitudes corresponding to graph ISR1 in figure (
8
)
read:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(203)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(204)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

Diagram IS2

The amplitude for diagram IS2, when the photon is longitudinally polarized,
reads:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}

(205)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

In the transverse case, we obtain:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{+}(p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(206)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

In the above expressions, we have used the definition:

\displaystyle\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}}\big(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}\big)\;.

(207)

Diagram IS3

The amplitudes for the production of a longitudinally or transversely
polarized virtual photon read, respectively:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS3}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(208)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS3}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)

(209)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

Diagram IS4

Finally, for the instantaneous
q\to\gamma^{*}gq
splitting, we obtain
the following result for the longitudinally resp. transversely polarized
amplitudes:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS4}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}\Bigg[\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}iA^{i}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})\big(\delta^{i\eta}+i\sigma^{i\eta}\big)

(210)

\displaystyle+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{q}^{i}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})\big(\delta^{i\eta}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}i\sigma^{i\eta}\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS4}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\eta\lambda}+\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)i\sigma^{\eta\lambda}\Bigg]

(211)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

5.2
Final-state radiation

Figure 9:
The four diagrams corresponding to real gluon emission
in the final state.

Diagram FS1

The amplitude corresponding to the first final-state radiation diagram
in figure (
9
) gives, in the longitudinally polarized case:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(212)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and when the photon is transversely polarized:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(213)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

In the above, we have introduced:

\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}\equiv\frac{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)\;.

(214)

For later purposes (for instance the high-energy resummation in section
(
8
)), it can be convenient to go to transverse coordinate
space, writing (obtained by inverting (
401
)):

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\;,

(215)

\displaystyle=e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})

such that:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(216)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(217)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Diagram FS2

The amplitudes read:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS2}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(218)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\frac{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}\!-\!\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}{\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{q^{+}p_{3}^{+}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}\!+\!\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}\!+\!\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS2}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{q^{+}}\big)

(219)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\eta}}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\lambda}}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\lambda}}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{q^{+}p_{3}^{+}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}\!+\!\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}\!+\!\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\cdot\mathbf{x}}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Diagram FS3

The amplitudes corresponding to Feynman graph FS3 (fig. (
9
))
for the emission of a longitudinally or transversely polarized photon
read, respectively:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(220)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}

(221)

\displaystyle\times\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{q^{+}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

With the help of the relation:

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot\mathbf{p}_{3}}

\displaystyle=e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\;,

(222)

the above amplitudes can also be cast in coordinate space, yielding:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)

(223)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{q^{+}}\big)

(224)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Diagram FS4

The last diagrams we consider correspond to the instantaneous
q\to q\gamma^{*}g

splitting after the initial quark has interacted with the shockwave.
In the longitudinal case, we find:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS4}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}\!+\!\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}\Bigg[\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+})}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}(\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta}\!-\!i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta})

(225)

\displaystyle+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}\Big(\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta}\!+\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta}\Big)\Bigg]\!\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}\!-\!1\big)\;,

while we obtain for the emission of a transversely polarized virtual photon:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS4}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\eta\lambda}

(226)

\displaystyle+i\sigma^{\eta\lambda}\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{x}\cdot(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

For later purposes, it can be useful to write:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\;,

(227)

\displaystyle=e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\;,

such that:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS4}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{\bar{\eta}}(\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta}-i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta})

(228)

\displaystyle+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}\Big(\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}i\sigma^{\bar{\eta}\eta}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS4}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\delta^{\eta\lambda}+i\sigma^{\eta\lambda}\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]

(229)

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)\cdot\mathbf{z}}\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

6
Infrared safety in the initial state

In this section, we show that two of the initial-state radiation diagrams
(figure
8
), namely
\mathrm{IS1}
and
\mathrm{IS2}
,
lead to infrared divergences on the level of the cross section, i.e.
after squaring and integrating over the outgoing gluon. These divergences
stem from the kinematic region in which the gluon, radiated from the
incoming quark, is collinear to its emitter. First, we will evaluate
the divergent integrals over the transverse momentum of the outgoing
gluon
\mathbf{p}_{3}
using dimensional regularization. The collinear
singularities will be parameterized by the poles
\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}
.
We shall then demonstrate how these poles can be absorbed into the
DGLAP evolution equations of the incoming quark.

6.1
Collinear divergences from initial-state radiation

Real corrections to the NLO cross section are obtained by computing
the
2\to 3
partonic amplitudes, multiplying them with their complex
conjugate, and integrating the additional parton out (see below, subsection

6.2
). When considering the initial-state radiation
amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{0\eta}
(
203
)
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}
(
205
), we obtain
after promoting the reduced amplitudes to the full ones according
to formula (
47
), multiplying them with their complex
conjugate, and integrating over the gluon transverse momentum:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{0\eta}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}N_{c}C_{F}}{M^{2}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big)^{2}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)

(230)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Big(-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}}+1\big)\;.

Power counting teaches us that the integral over the gluon transverse
momentum with respect to its emitter contains a collinear divergence:
4
4

4

Ultraviolet divergences
\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}\to\infty
are cut
off by the phase.

\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{\mathrm{coll}}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)+\mathcal{O}(\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}})\;,

(231)

which is easily proven with the help of the Schwinger trick (
399
).
Therefore, in the collinear limit
\boldsymbol{\ell}\to 0
, the third
line in formula (
230
) can be approximated by:

\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}\to\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\;.

(232)

With these simplifications, the collinearly divergent part of eq.
(
230
) can be rewritten as:
5
5

5

Of course, the above notation is somewhat symbolic, as the square
of the leading order part
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}|^{2}
still
contains the integrals over
\mathbf{x}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}
,
which also appear in the phase
e^{-i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}
.

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{(0,\lambda)\eta}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{(0,\lambda)\eta}\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{coll.}}{=}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0,\lambda}|^{2}\times-\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)

(233)

\displaystyle\times\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)\;.

It is easily verified that the above result also holds in the case
of a transversely polarized virtual photon, hence the index
(0,\lambda)
.
This implies that we have extracted a universal collinear factor,
which is independent of the details of the leading-order process.
It is, therefore, natural to associate it with quantum fluctuations
of the incoming quark before it participates in the hard process.
Indeed, in the next section we demonstrate that the collinear pole
can be absorbed into the DGLAP evolution of the quark PDF.

6.2
From parton to hadron level

On the partonic level, the amplitudes for the real NLO corrections
are transformed into contributions to the cross section using the
following formula:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\hat{\sigma}_{\mathrm{real}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q},\vec{p}_{3})2\pi\delta(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}-p_{1}^{+}-q^{+}-p_{3}^{+})\frac{1}{D-2}\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{real}}\big|^{2}\;,

(234)

where we used the short-hand notation:

\mathrm{PS}(\vec{k})=\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}\mathrm{d}^{D-2}\mathbf{k}}{(2\pi)^{D-3}2k^{+}}\;,

(235)

for the phase-space integration measure. As discussed in section
2
,
eq. (
6
), the partonic cross section needs to
be transformed into a hadronic one by convolving with the leading-order
quark PDF
f_{q}^{(0)}
, and taking the CGC average:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{real}}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{p}^{+}}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\langle\mathrm{d}\hat{\sigma}_{\mathrm{real}}\rangle\;.

(236)

Finally, the radiated gluon is integrated out in order to obtain the
real radiative corrections to the full NLO cross section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{NLO}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{virtual}}+\int\mathrm{d}^{D-1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{real}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D-1}p_{3}}\;.

(237)

We shall now apply the procedure above to transform eq. (
233
)
into a contribution to the NLO cross section. Promoting to the partonic
cross section, performing the convolution with the PDF, and averaging
over the semiclassical background fields, we find:
6
6

6

Note that we do not distinguish anymore between the polarization states
of the virtual photon, since the collinear initial-state radiation
is insensitive to them.

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}^{D\!-\!1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS1,2}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D\!-\!1}p_{3}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{coll.}}{=}\!\!\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{p}^{+}}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\!\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{(2\pi)^{D\!-\!3}2p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big)^{\!2}\Big(\big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{\!2}\!+\!D\!-\!3\Big)

(238)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{2p_{0}^{+}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})2\pi\delta(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}-p_{1}^{+}-q^{+}-p_{3}^{+})\frac{1}{D-2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle

\displaystyle\times-\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)\;.

After a change of variables in the integral over gluon plus-momentum,
introducing
\xi=p_{0}^{+}/p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}
in function of which
p_{3}^{+}=p_{0}^{+}\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}

with
\mathrm{d}\xi/\xi=\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}/p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}
, and remembering
the leading-order definition of projectile quark momentum fraction

x_{p}=p_{0}^{+}/p_{p}^{+}
, we finally obtain:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}^{D-1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS1,2}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D-1}p_{3}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{coll.}}{=}\int_{x_{p}}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

(239)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle

\displaystyle\times-\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)\;.

The upper bound
p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})>\xi

in the integral stems from the rapidity cutoff
p_{3}^{+}>k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
,
while the requirement
\xi>x_{p}
comes from the domain of the PDF.
To cast it in a form in which one can recognize DGLAP, let us start
by splitting up the integration:

\displaystyle\int_{x_{p}}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

(240)

\displaystyle=\Big(\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi+\int_{1}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\Big)\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\;.

The next step is to make use of the definition of the plus-distribution:

\displaystyle\int_{z}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{f(\xi)}{(1-\xi)_{+}}

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{z}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{f(\xi)-f(1)}{1-\xi}-f(1)\int_{0}^{z}\frac{\mathrm{d}\xi}{1-\xi}\;,

(241)

using which the first integration in (
240
) can be
written as:

\displaystyle\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

\displaystyle=\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)+x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d\xi}\frac{2}{1-\xi}\;.

(242)

The second integral in (
240
) is the one that contains
the divergence for
p_{3}^{+}\to 0
, or equivalently,
\xi\to 1
.
In this limit, one can write:

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

\displaystyle\simeq x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\int_{1}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{2}{1-\xi}\;.

(243)

Combining (
242
) and (
243
), eq.
(
240
) eventually becomes:

\displaystyle\int_{x_{p}}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{1-\xi}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

(244)

\displaystyle=\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)+x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}/(p_{0}^{+}+k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})}\mathrm{d\xi}\frac{2}{1-\xi}\;,

\displaystyle=\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi\Big(\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}+\frac{3}{2}\delta(1-\xi)\Big)\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)+\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+2\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\;.

In the last line of the above formula, one can recognize the Altarelli-Parisi

q\to q
splitting function:

P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)=C_{F}\Big(\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}+\frac{3}{2}\delta(1-\xi)\Big)\;.

(245)

We, therefore, have shown that eq. (
239
) can
be rewritten as:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}^{D-1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS1,2}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D-1}p_{3}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{coll.}}{=}\!\!\Bigg[\!\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\!+\!C_{F}\Big(\!-\!\frac{3}{2}\!+\!2\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\Bigg]

(246)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle

\displaystyle\times-\alpha_{s}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)\;.

However, the above pole, stemming from real gluon radiation in the
initial-state, is not the first collinear singularity we have encountered
in this work. Remember that in subsection
3.6
, we discovered
collinear poles stemming from the quark field-strength renormalization:

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}^{\dagger}\big)\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)

(247)

\displaystyle\times x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle\;.

Adding (
246
) and (
247
), one obtains:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}^{D-1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS1,2}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D-1}p_{3}}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}+\big(\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}^{\dagger}\big)\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}

(248)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\alpha_{s}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\mathrm{ln}\big(\mu^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\big)\Big)\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

\displaystyle+\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Big(\mathrm{ln}\frac{e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\Bigg]\;.

In the above equation, the only collinear singularity left is proportional
to the leading-order squared amplitude times the quark PDF convolved
with the Altarelli-Parisi splitting function. The final step is to
notice that the (
\overline{\mathrm{MS}}
-) quark PDF at NLO:
f_{q}^{(1)}
,
is related to the leading order one
f_{q}^{(0)}
as follows:
7
7

7

In principle, eq. (
249
) contains an additional term
proportional to the gluon PDF. The corresponding collinear pole should
cancel with initial-state radiation in the
g+A\to\gamma^{*}+q+\bar{q}

contribution to the cross section. The analysis of the gluon channel
is left for future work, see also the discussion around fig.
10
.

\displaystyle x_{p}f_{q}^{(1)}(x_{p},\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})

(249)

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln 4\pi\Big)\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}\xi}{\xi}P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)+\mathcal{O}(\alpha_{s}^{2})\;.

Therefore, we can rewrite the LO cross section as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}+\mathrm{DGLAP}}+\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln 4\pi\Big)\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}\xi}{\xi}P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\;

(250)

\displaystyle\times\frac{2\pi}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{D-2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle\;,

where we defined
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}+\mathrm{DGLAP}}

as the leading-order cross section convolved with the NLO quark PDF:

\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}+\mathrm{DGLAP}}=x_{p}f_{q}^{(1)}\big(x_{p},\mu^{2}\big)\frac{2\pi}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{D-2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle\;.

(251)

Adding (
248
) and (
250
), we finally
obtain:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}+\int\mathrm{d}^{D-1}\vec{p}_{3}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS1,2}}}{\mathrm{d}^{D-1}p_{3}}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}+\big(\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{IS}}^{\dagger}\big)\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}+\mathrm{DGLAP}}+

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}\;,

(252)

with:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}

\displaystyle\equiv\alpha_{s}\Bigg[\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\mu^{2}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)

(253)

\displaystyle+C_{F}\Big(\mathrm{ln}\frac{e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle\;.

The above result proves that, at least at NLO accuracy, the hybrid
dilute-dense factorization Ansatz employed in this work holds, as
a collinear PDF is sufficient to absorb all collinear singularities
stemming from initial-state radiation.

7
Infrared safety in the final state

7.1
Jet algorithm

For the leading-order and virtual contributions to the cross section, the outgoing
quark can be trivially related to the jet which it will initiate.
Indeed, it is sufficient to assume that the jet will be centered around
the outgoing quark, such that
\vec{p}_{\mathrm{jet}}=\vec{p}_{1}
.
However, for the real NLO corrections, this is not true anymore. Instead,
we need to specify what we mean by a jet, using a specific jet algorithm.
This algorithm allows one to distinguish the case in which the quark
and the gluon are grouped inside the same jet, when the reaction has
a similar topology as the leading-order one, from the situation where
both the quark and the gluon each initiate a separate jet. Since our
main purpose here is to prove final-state infrared safety, we can
limit ourselves to the following straightforward algorithm: the outgoing
quark and gluon are grouped inside the same jet when their momenta
satisfy the condition:

\displaystyle\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{|\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3}|}\Big|\frac{\mathbf{p}_{1}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big|

\displaystyle<R\;.

(254)

The parameter
R
is known as the jet radius parameter and is bounded
by
0<R<1
. Moreover, for our purposes it is sufficient to work in
the so-called narrow-jet limit
R\to 0
, suppressing all positive
powers of
R
in the calculation.
8
8

8

We note that, in this limit, our jet definition becomes equivalent to the Cambridge/Aachen algorithm
Dokshitzer:1997in
;
Wobisch:1998wt
In practice, this means that there
is only a single configuration where the quark and gluon are part
of the same jet, namely when the gluon is radiated collinearly to
the quark in the final state. Note that, in this work, we always consider
the case where the observed jet is initiated by the quark and the
gluon is integrated out, whether the latter forms its own jet or belongs
to the quark one. For later phenomenological applications, our intermediate

q+A\to\gamma^{*}+q+g
results can be directly used to calculate
the contribution to the cross section where the observed jet stems
from the gluon, while the quark is either part of this same jet or stays undetected. Likewise, if desired, it always remains possible to
employ a more sophisticated jet algorithm.

Let us start by adding and squaring the amplitudes corresponding to
Feynman diagrams
\mathrm{FS2}
(
219
) and
\mathrm{FS3}

(
221
), starting with the transverse case:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\lambda}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\!g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}C_{F}N_{c}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{\!2}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\!\Big(\!\big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\!\big)^{\!2}\!+\!D\!-\!3\!\Big)\!\Big(\!\big(1\!+\!2\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{\!2}\!+\!D\!-\!3\!\Big)

(255)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\Bigg[\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathbf{q}-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}(\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;,

where the spinor trace was evaluated with the help of the relations
in sec.
B
. Similarly, in the longitudinal case:

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}C_{F}N_{c}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)\frac{1}{M^{2}}\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}

(256)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\frac{\big((p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})\mathbf{q}-q^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\big)^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}-M^{2}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}-\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

From the structure of the above expressions, it is clear that both
of them contain a divergence in the limit
p_{1}^{+}\mathbf{p}_{3}-p_{3}^{+}\mathbf{p}_{1}\to 0
,
i.e. when the gluon is radiated collinearly to its parent quark. As
discussed in the previous paragraph, this implies that eqs. (
255
)
and (
256
) will contribute to the scenario in which the
jet algorithm groups the quark and gluon inside the same jet.

7.2
Gluon inside the jet

Let us first consider the case when the quark and the gluon are grouped
inside the same jet. Integrating over the gluon momentum, the jet
algorithm (
254
) is then imposed by adding the following
step function to the integrand:

\displaystyle\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})

\displaystyle=\theta\Bigg((\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})^{2}R^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{p}_{1}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Bigg)\;.

(257)

Moreover, the quark momentum
\vec{p}_{1}
is not the correct physical
parameter anymore, but rather we should work with the jet momentum

\vec{p}_{j}\equiv\vec{p}_{1}+\vec{p}_{3}
. With the help of the
intermediate relations

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{k_{\perp}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}}{=}\frac{(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})(q^{+}+p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{(p_{j}^{+}+q^{+})p_{j}^{+}}

(258)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)^{2}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}q^{+}}\big((p_{j}^{+}+q^{+})\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}^{2}+p_{j}^{+}M^{2}\big)}\;,

and

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}}{=}\Big(\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)^{2}\;,

(259)

one then obtains, writing
\boldsymbol{\ell}=\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}
:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\mathrm{\lambda}}|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})

(260)

\displaystyle\overset{\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}}{=}\!\!g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}C_{F}N_{c}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\!\Big)^{\!2}\!8(p_{j}^{+}\!-\!p_{3}^{+})(p_{j}^{+}\!+\!q^{+})\!\Big(\!\big(2\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\!-\!1\!\big)^{2}\!+\!D\!-\!3\!\Big)\!\Big(\!\big(\!1\!+\!2\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{q^{+}}\!\big)^{2}\!+\!D\!-\!3\!\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg[\frac{(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})p_{3}^{+}}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}q^{+}}\big((p_{j}^{+}+q^{+})\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}^{2}+p_{j}^{+}M^{2}\big)}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{j}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\times\theta\Bigg(\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{(p_{j}^{+})^{4}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}\Bigg)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{j})\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

In the above expression, we changed the integration variable from

\mathbf{p}_{3}
to
\boldsymbol{\ell}\equiv\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}
,
and introduced the analogues of the momentum combinations (
30
),
now with respect to the jet momentum:

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{p}_{j}-p_{j}^{+}\mathbf{q}}{q^{+}+p_{j}^{+}}\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\mathbf{k}_{j\perp}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathbf{p}_{j}+\mathbf{q}\;.

(261)

Similarly, in the longitudinally polarized case, we have:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0}\big|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})

(262)

\displaystyle\overset{\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}}{=}\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}}{M^{2}}C_{F}N_{c}8(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})(p_{j}^{+}+q^{+})\Big(\big(1+\frac{2(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+D-3\Big)\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg[\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\frac{(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}^{2}-p_{j}^{+}M^{2}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}q^{+}}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{j\perp}^{2}+p_{j}^{+}M^{2}\big)}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{j}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{j}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\times\theta\Bigg(\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{(p_{j}^{+})^{4}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}\Bigg)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{q}+\mathbf{p}_{j})\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

The transverse integrals in eq. (
260
) can be evaluated
with the help of the following identities, which are easily proven
in dimensional regularization:

\displaystyle\mu^{4-D}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D-2}\boldsymbol{\ell}}{(2\pi)^{D-2}}\frac{\theta(\mathbf{b}-\boldsymbol{\ell})}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big[\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\ln(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu^{2}}{b^{2}})\Big]+\mathcal{O}(b,\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}})\;,

(263)

\displaystyle\mu^{4-D}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D-2}\boldsymbol{\ell}}{(2\pi)^{D-2}}\frac{\theta(\mathbf{b}-\boldsymbol{\ell})}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+m)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4\pi m}\Big[\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\ln(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu^{2}}{b^{2}})\Big]+\mathcal{O}(b,\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}})\;,

\displaystyle\mu^{4-D}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D-2}\boldsymbol{\ell}}{(2\pi)^{D-2}}\frac{\theta(\mathbf{b}-\boldsymbol{\ell})}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+m)^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4\pi m^{2}}\Big[\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\ln(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu^{2}}{b^{2}})\Big]+\mathcal{O}(b,\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}})\;,

where we should note remind the reader that we work in the narrow-cone
approximation
R\to 0
, keeping only negative power-like or logarithmic
dependencies on
R
. We also expand
D-3=1-2\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}

from the Dirac structure
\big(2\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}-1\big)^{2}+D-3
.
In both the transverse (
260
) and longitudinally
(
262
) polarized case, we obtain a result proportional
to the corresponding leading-order amplitude:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0,\lambda}|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})

(264)

\displaystyle=\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0,\lambda}\big|^{2}\times-\alpha_{s}C_{F}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\ln\big(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu^{2}(p_{j}^{+})^{4}}{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})^{2}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}}\big)-\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{(p_{j}^{+})^{2}+(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}-1\big)^{2}+1\Big)+\mathcal{O}(\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}})\;.

Note that, in the transverse case, the structure
\big(1\!+\!2\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}\!+\!D\!-\!3

is absorbed into
\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1+2}}^{\mathrm{T}}\big|^{2}
,
which hence stays
D
-dimensional.

The next step is to integrate over the gluon plus-momentum. However,
due to the shift
\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}
dictated
by the jet algorithm, an additional dependence on
p_{3}^{+}
is
introduced on the level of the cross section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{in}}^{\mathrm{T},\mathrm{L}}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{D-2}\mathbf{p}_{3}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2p_{3}^{+}}\frac{1}{2p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{1}^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{D-2}\mathbf{p}_{1}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}q^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{2}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2q^{+}}

(265)

\displaystyle\times 2\pi\delta(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}-p_{1}^{+}-q^{+}-p_{3}^{+})\frac{1}{D-2}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0,\lambda}|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})\;,

\displaystyle\overset{\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3}}{=}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{D-2}\mathbf{p}_{3}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2p_{3}^{+}}\frac{1}{2p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{j}^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{D-2}\mathbf{p}_{j}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}\frac{\mathrm{d}q^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{2}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2q^{+}}

\displaystyle\times 2\pi\delta(p_{0}^{+}-p_{j}^{+}-q^{+})\frac{1}{D-2}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0,\lambda}|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3},\vec{p}_{3})\;,

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{j}^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{D-2}\mathbf{p}_{j}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2p_{j}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}q^{+}\mathrm{\mathrm{d}}^{2}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{D-1}2q^{+}}2\pi\delta(p_{0}^{+}-p_{j}^{+}-q^{+})\frac{1}{D-2}

\displaystyle\times\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{(2\pi)^{D-3}2p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{(p_{j}^{+}-p_{3}^{+})}\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0,\lambda}|^{2}\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{j}-\vec{p}_{3},\vec{p}_{3})\;.

Evaluating the plus-momentum integral, we finally obtain:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{in}}^{\mathrm{T,L}}

\displaystyle=\!\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{T},\mathrm{L}}\,\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\!\Bigg[\!\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}\!+\!\ln\big(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu^{2}}{\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}}\big)\!\Big)\!\Big(\frac{3}{4}\!-\!\ln\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\!+\!\frac{13}{4}\!-\!\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\!-\!\ln^{2}\!\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Bigg]\;,

(266)

where
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{T},\mathrm{L}}
is
the leading-order amplitude in terms of the jet instead of the quark.
Adding the above result to the ‘final-state’ part of
the virtual field-strength renormalization corrections (
159
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{\mathcal{Z}}_{\mathrm{FS}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{coll}}}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{3}{4}+\mathrm{ln}\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;,

(267)

where we can identify the quark momentum with the jet one, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{in}}\!+\!\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{\mathcal{Z}}_{\mathrm{FS}}}

\displaystyle=\!\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\!\times\!\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\ln\big(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}}\big)\Big(\frac{3}{4}-\ln\!\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\!+\!\frac{13}{4}\!-\!\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\!-\!\ln^{2}\!\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\!\Bigg]\;.

(268)

We omitted the labels indicating the photon polarization since the
above result does not depend on it. The above expression is an important
result, demonstrating the cancellation of final-state collinear divergencies
in our cross section. There is, however, one loose end in the form
of a double logarithm in the rapidity cutoff
k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
.
Since high-energy resummation only involves single large logarithms,
this term is unphysical and needs to cancel in the final cross section.
In the next subsection, we will show that the unphysical double logarithm
cancels when adding the contribution of a soft gluon just outside
the jet.

7.3
Gluon outside the jet

When the gluon and its parent quark each form a distinct jet, the
quark momentum can be directly identified with the one of the jet:

\vec{p}_{j}=\vec{p}_{1}
. The jet function becomes, introducing

\boldsymbol{\ell}\equiv\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}
:

\displaystyle 1-\theta_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1}\to\vec{p}_{j},\vec{p}_{3})

\displaystyle=\theta\Big((p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}\Big(\frac{\mathbf{p}_{j}}{p_{j}^{+}}-\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}-(\mathbf{p}_{j}+\mathbf{p}_{3})^{2}R^{2}\Big)\;,

(269)

\displaystyle=\theta\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}+p_{j}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)^{2}R^{2}\Big)\;,

\displaystyle\approx\theta\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}\Big)\;,

where the last equality holds in the narrow-jet limit
R\to 0
. Integrating
over the gluon momentum in (
255
) then gives:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\lambda\eta}|^{2}\big(1-\mathrm{\theta}_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})\big)

(270)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}C_{F}N_{c}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg[\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\frac{p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\times\theta\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}8p_{j}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{j}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{j}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}+\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp})\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

From experience
Taels:2022tza
, we expect that a similar unphysical
double logarithm as the one in eq. (
268
) will appear
from the phase-space integration over the gluon, in the kinematical
region where the latter is just outside the jet with small plus-momentum,
i.e. when simultaneously:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\ell}=\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{j}

\displaystyle\to 0\quad\mathrm{and}\quad p_{3}^{+}\to 0\;.

(271)

In this regime, eq. (
270
) can be approximated by:

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathrm{soft}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}|^{2}\big(1-\mathrm{\theta}_{\mathrm{in}}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{p}_{3})\big)

(272)

\displaystyle=|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}|^{2}4\alpha_{s}C_{F}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\theta\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{j}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}\Big)\;,

\displaystyle=|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}|^{2}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(2\ln\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\ln\frac{c_{0}}{R|\mathbf{p}_{j}||\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}|}+\ln^{2}\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\;,

where we used the identity (with
c_{0}=2e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}
):

\displaystyle\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\theta(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\mathbf{b}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{b}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}\ell}{\ell}J_{0}\big(\ell|\mathbf{x}|\big)=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln\frac{c_{0}}{b|\mathbf{x}|}\;.

(273)

and where we suppressed the polarization labels since the same relation
holds in the longitudinal case. Promoting the result (
272
)
to the cross-section level, and adding it to eq. (
268
),
we end up with:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{in}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{\mathcal{Z}}_{\mathrm{FS}}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{out,soft}}

(274)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\frac{3}{4}\ln\big(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}}\big)+\frac{13}{4}-\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}+\ln\frac{p_{j}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\ln\frac{c_{0}}{2\pi\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\Bigg]\;,

in which the pathological double logarithm has cancelled.

8
High-energy resummation

In sections
4
,
6
, and
7
, we have demonstrated that the different ultraviolet- and collinear divergences
encountered in our calculation all cancel or, in the case of initial-state
collinear poles, can be absorbed into the DGLAP evolution of the incoming
quark. We are now in a position to treat the remaining high-energy
or rapidity divergences, which either stem from the
k_{3}^{+}\rightarrow 0

limit in the gluon loop for virtual diagrams, either from the
p_{3}^{+}\rightarrow 0

region in the gluon phase-space integration of real NLO contributions
in the cross section. In both cases, they are regularized with a cutoff

k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
and come in the form of single large logarithms.
To be more precise, let us formally separate the integral over gluon
plus-momentum in the (fixed-order) NLO cross section as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{NLO}}

\displaystyle=\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\mathrm{d}\tilde{\sigma}_{\mathrm{NLO}}\;.

(275)

Introducing the high-energy factorization scale
k_{f}^{+}
, we can rewrite the equation above:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{NLO}}

\displaystyle=\!\!\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\!\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\!+\!\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\!\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\mathrm{d}\tilde{\sigma}_{\mathrm{NLO}}\!-\!\theta(k_{f}^{+}\!-\!p_{3}^{+})\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\Big)

(276)

where
\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}
is the JIMWLK Hamiltonian, acting
on the target averages in the leading-order cross section. We will
show in the following subsections that the above subtraction method
works, namely that all the rapidity-divergent contributions in our
NLO cross section can be combined into the first term of the above
equation, such that the second term is a completely finite rapidity-subtracted
cross section. Explicitly, the action of the JIMWLK Hamiltonian on
the dipole operator is:

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha_{s}N_{c}}{2\pi^{2}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{2}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})^{2}}\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x^{\prime}}\mathbf{x}}-s_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}}\big\rangle\;.

(277)

Moreover, at fixed order, the target average of the Wilson-line structure
in the leading-order cross section (
46
) does not contain
any evolution, hence we label it with a zero:

\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle_{0}\;.

(278)

However, with JIMWLK we can resum the high-energy leading logarithms

Y_{f}^{+}\equiv\ln(k_{f}^{+}/k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+})
as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{Y_{f}^{+}}\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle_{Y_{f}^{+}}

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Big\rangle_{Y_{f}^{+}}\;.

(279)

Integrating the above equation, we obtain:

\displaystyle\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle_{Y_{f}^{+}}

\displaystyle=\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle_{0}+\int_{0}^{Y_{f}^{+}}\mathrm{d}Y^{+}\Big\langle\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Big\rangle_{Y^{+}}\;,

(280)

\displaystyle=\big\langle s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big\rangle_{0}+Y_{f}^{+}\Big\langle\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Big\rangle+{\cal O}(\alpha_{s}^{2})\;.

In the last line, we have performed a fixed-order expansion, where
we should note that the JIMWLK Hamiltonian is of order
\alpha_{s}

and that the dependence of the target average on the rapidity scale
is not specified since it is a higher-order effect. We can, therefore,
write the analogue of eq. (
250
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO+JIMWLK}}-\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\;.

(281)

Combining eqs. (
281
) and (
276
),
one then obtains the final result:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\!+\!\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{NLO}}

\displaystyle=\!\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO+JIMWLK}}\!+\!\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\mathrm{d}\tilde{\sigma}_{\mathrm{NLO}}\!-\!\theta(k_{f}^{+}\!-\!p_{3}^{+})\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\Big)\;.

(282)

8.1
Leftovers from UV and collinear subtractions

In section
4
, we have demonstrated how the sum of the
different UV counterterms in the virtual amplitudes yields a result
which is free from any UV poles. There are, however, still rapidity
divergences left, regularized by the cutoff
k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
.
On the level of the cross section, we have for the longitudinally
polarized photon production (
200
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\mathrm{L}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{L}}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg]\;.

(283)

Following the usual high-energy resummation procedure, we will extract
from above cross section the terms enhanced by a large rapidity or
high-energy logarithm, and cut the (implicit) integration over the
gluon plus-momentum off at the rapidity factorization scale
k_{f}^{+}
:

\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\begin{aligned} \mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\mathrm{L}}&=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{L}}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\frac{2\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;.\end{aligned}

(284)

We obtain the same result when the photon is transversally polarized
(
201
):

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}^{\mathrm{T}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\mathrm{T}}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\frac{2\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\ln\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;.

(285)

Let us now turn to the real NLO corrections that contributed to the
DGLAP evolution of the incoming quark (
252
). Keeping
only the high-energy logarithms, we find:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\times\mathrm{ln}\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\mathrm{ln}\frac{e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}}{\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}\pi(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\;.

(286)

irrespective of the polarization of the photon.

Finally, there are leftover rapidity logarithms after the cancellation
of collinear divergences in the final state by the jet algorithm (
274
),
namely:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\times\mathrm{ln}\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\ln\frac{c_{0}}{2\pi\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\;.

(287)

Adding (
284
) or (
285
) to (
286
)
and (
287
), we obtain both for transverse and longitudinally
polarized photons:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\Big(\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\frac{2\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\;.

(288)

It is worth remarking that, in the above result, the arbitrary scale

\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}
stemming from the quark field-strength renormalization
has cancelled. This is not surprising, since eq. (
288
)
contains all contributions (
\mathrm{UV}
,
\mathrm{IS}
,
\mathrm{FS}

and their conjugates) from the one-loop result for
\mathcal{Z}

(
159
), which is independent from
\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}
.

8.2
Virtual contributions

We will show in the following section (see eq. (
329
))
that, although they are individually divergent in the limit of vanishing
gluon plus-momentum, the sums of the UV-subtracted amplitudes
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0,\lambda}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0,\lambda}

and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0,\lambda}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0,\lambda}

do not contribute any rapidity logarithms to the cross section, nor
is there any dependence on the factorization scale
k_{f}^{+}
. Moreover,
the high-energy logarithms stemming from the field-strength renormalization
diagrams (
159
) were already taken into account in the previous
subsection. It is, then, very easy to see that the only virtual diagrams
left with a high-energy logarithm are the subtracted diagrams
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{0,\lambda}

(
62
) , (
63
) and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{0,\lambda}

(
68
), (
69
). Indeed, using that in
the limit
k^{+}\to 0
the Dirac structure (
51
) becomes:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{SE}}^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}(p_{0}^{+})

\displaystyle=-4\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big)^{2}\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\;,

(289)

we obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=4\alpha_{s}\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}

(290)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;,

and:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=-4\alpha_{s}\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}

(291)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

as well as:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-4\alpha_{s}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{1}{M}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}

(292)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;,

and:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=-4\alpha_{s}\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}

(293)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(t^{c}U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\Bigg]\;.

Cutting off the logarithmic integral over
k^{+}
at the rapidity
factorization scale
k_{f}^{+}
, the above amplitudes contribute as follows to the cross section:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{SE2+3}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}

(294)

\displaystyle\times 4\alpha_{s}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})+A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(N_{c}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+2C_{F}\Big)-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})2C_{F}(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1)\Bigg]\;.

8.3
Real contributions

The real contributions to JIMWLK that were not yet taken into account
in subsection
8.1
, are the interferences
between the initial-state radiation amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}

and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}
(
203
), (
204
),
(
208
), and (
209
) on the one hand, and the final-state
amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}

(
216
), (
217
), (
223
)
and (
224
) on the other.

Indeed, one obtains:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS1}+3}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{M}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}{}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(295)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;,

and

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1+3}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{M}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(296)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\eta}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

Similarly, when the photon is transversally polarized:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS1+3}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=2\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(297)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;,

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{FS1+3}}^{\lambda\eta}

\displaystyle=-2\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\bar{\lambda}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(298)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}iA^{\eta}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})t^{c}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}-1\big)\;.

On the level of the cross section, the interference term due to the
above amplitudes is, independently of the photon polarization:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS-FS}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times 4\alpha_{s}\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})

(299)

\displaystyle\times\big(\frac{1}{N_{c}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-N_{c}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-2C_{F}\big)\;.

8.4
JIMWLK

We can now, finally, add the intermediate results (
288
),
(
294
), and (
299
):

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\Big(\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{SE2+3}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS-FS}}\Big)

(300)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times 4\alpha_{s}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})+A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(N_{c}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+2C_{F}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\big(\frac{1}{N_{c}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-N_{c}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-2C_{F}\big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{1}{4\pi}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\Big)2C_{F}(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1)\Bigg]\;.

The terms in the last line of the above expression can combined into:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\!\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\!-\!\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})

\displaystyle=\!-\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}\!-\!\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}\!+\!\ln\frac{4\pi\mu^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\!-\!\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\Big)\;,

(301)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln\pi\mu^{2}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\Big)\;,

where we used the expansion (
406
) of
\mathcal{A}_{0}
.
But in this result one recognizes:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon_{\mathrm{UV}}}+\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}+\ln\pi\mu^{2}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}\Big)

(302)

\displaystyle=\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}=\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\;,

such that

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\Big(\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{SE2+3}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS-FS}}\Big)

(303)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times 4\alpha_{s}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})+A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(N_{c}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+2C_{F}\Big)+\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}\big)\Bigg]\;.

Setting
\mathbf{x}=\mathbf{x}^{\prime}
in (
302
),
