we obtain a scaleless integral which in dimensional regularization
is, of course, equal to zero:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle=\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}=0\;.

(304)

We are, therefore, allowed to subtract the following, vanishing, contribution
from (
303
):

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})+A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Big)C_{F}(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1)

\displaystyle=0\;,

(305)

which gives:

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\Big(\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{SE2+3}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS-FS}}\Big)

(306)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}})

\displaystyle\times\!4\alpha_{s}N_{c}\!\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(\!A^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z})\!-\!\frac{1}{2}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})\!-\!\frac{1}{2}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z})\Big)\;.

The final step is to recognize the integration over Weizsäcker-Williams
fields, in the last line, as the BK kernel:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{z}}\Big(A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})-\frac{1}{2}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})-\frac{1}{2}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Big)

(307)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{2}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})^{2}}\;,

such that we recover the JIMWLK evolution of a dipole (
277
):

\displaystyle\lim_{k^{+}\to 0}\Big(\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{UV}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{SE2+3}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS-FS}}\Big)

(308)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}}{s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1}\times\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times-\frac{\alpha_{s}N_{c}}{2\pi^{2}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{2}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})^{2}}(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}})

\displaystyle=\ln\frac{k_{f}^{+}}{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}\times\hat{H}_{\mathrm{JIMWLK}}\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\;.

Hence, we have proven the validity of eq. (
276
),
namely that all the rapidity-divergent NLO contributions combine into the JIMWLK Hamiltonian acting on the LO cross section. Therefore,
they can be subtracted and absorbed into the JIMWLK evolution of the
latter, according to eq. (
282
).

Some subtleties

But what about the other real contributions to the high-energy resummation?
First, the squared amplitudes
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}|^{2}

and
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}|^{2}
cause collinear initial- and
final state divergences when integrating over the gluon momentum.
Therefore, we have separately analyzed them in sections
6

and
7
, respectively, and showed how these collinear poles
cancel. The leftovers of these procedures still contain rapidity divergences
that are taken into account via eq. (
288
).

More subtle, however, are the cases of squared amplitudes
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}|^{2}

and
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}|^{2}
, as well as the interference
terms
2\mathrm{Re}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}

and
2\mathrm{Re}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}
.
They do not contribute to DGLAP or to the jet function and hence are
not taken into account in subsection
8.1

At first sight they seem to be missing from (
308
). To
solve this puzzle, we should revisit sections
6
and

7
, where we were not able to evaluate the transverse
integrations in amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}}

exactly and, instead, devised an approximation to extract their collinear
behavior. For definiteness, let us look again at amplitude
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0}

(the other cases are similar):

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}

(309)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

Clearly, this diagram will not lead to rapidity divergences, since
it disappears in the limit
p_{3}^{+}\to 0
. Introducing the transformation

\boldsymbol{\ell}=p_{3}^{+}/q^{+}\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}
and
only then taking the limit
p_{3}^{+}\to 0
allows us to study the
kinematics where all components of the gluon momentum tend to zero
simultaneously (the ‘genuine soft’ limit
\vec{p}_{3}\to 0
,
see e.g.
Taels:2022tza
):

\displaystyle\lim_{\vec{p}_{3}\to 0}\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle=\lim_{p_{3}^{+}\to 0}\frac{1}{M}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}

(310)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}}\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\frac{\big(p_{3}^{+}\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}{\big(p_{3}^{+}\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}/q^{+}+p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)=0\;.

It follows that
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0}
cannot generate
logarithmic divergences of the form
\ln p^{+}/k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}
:
neither rapidity ones, nor soft ones when the gluon transverse momentum
tends to zero simultaneously with its plus-momentum component. However,
in the approximation (
232
), we have set
\boldsymbol{\ell}\to 0

to extract the collinear behavior:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}

\displaystyle\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}=\frac{1}{M}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\eta\bar{\eta}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}

(311)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{z}}\big(U_{\mathbf{x}}U_{\mathbf{z}}^{\dagger}t^{c}U_{\mathbf{z}}-t^{c}\big)\;.

But the above expression
does
lead to a rapidity divergence,
in particular exactly the same as the one from amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}^{0\eta}
:

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{3}^{+}\to 0}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{p_{3}^{+}\to 0}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}^{0\eta}\;.

(312)

Thus, the reason that, in section
299
, we only need
to consider the interference terms
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+3}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+3}^{\dagger}
,
is because the rapidity divergences from
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1+3}}|^{2}

and
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1+3}}|^{2}
are accounted for in eq.

288
. The rapidity-divergent contributions
from
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}|^{2}
and
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}|^{2}

are ‘physical’, while the others:
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}|^{2}
,

|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}|^{2}
,
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}
,
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}

are
mimicked
by the artificially large logarithmic terms
|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}|^{2}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}
,

|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}}|^{2}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}
,
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}
,
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}}^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}}\Big|_{\mathrm{coll.}}
.

9
Next-to-leading order cross section

We are now ready to present the full NLO cross section for our process.
It is given by the following sum of separately finite contributions:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO+NLO}}^{pA\to\gamma^{*}+\mathrm{jet}+X}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO+DGLAP+JIMWLK}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{virtual}}+\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{real}}\;.

(313)

The first part of the cross section (
313
)
is the leading-order one, eq. (
46
), where the quark
PDF is evolved at least one step with DGLAP (
251
),
and where the target average of the dipole is evolved at least one
step with JIMWLK (
281
). For all the other terms, it
is understood that the subtraction of high-energy leading logarithms
has been performed according to (
282
) in the previous
section.

The term
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}
(
274
),
after performing the rapidity subtraction, reads:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{LO}}\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Big(\frac{3}{4}\ln\big(\frac{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}{\mathbf{p}_{j}^{2}R^{2}}\big)+\frac{13}{4}-\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\;.

(314)

Similarly, the contribution to
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}
is
obtained after subtracting the large high-energy logarithm from (
253
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}\big\langle\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}\big|^{2}\big\rangle

(315)

\displaystyle\times\alpha_{s}\Big(\int_{x_{p}}^{1}\mathrm{d}\xi P_{qq}^{(0)}(\xi)\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}f_{q}^{(0)}\big(\frac{x_{p}}{\xi}\big)\!-\!\frac{3}{2}C_{F}\mathrm{ln}\frac{\mu^{2}}{4\pi e^{-\gamma_{\scriptscriptstyle{E}}}\mu_{\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{2}}x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\!\Big)\!\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{\mu^{2}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{x}^{\prime})^{2}}\;.

The term
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{virtual}}
, the finite leftovers
of the virtual diagrams are collected after cancelling the UV divergences,
and after absorbing the collinear divergences inside the jet definition
and the DGLAP evolution. Moreover, the rapidity subtraction procedure
has been performed explicitly, hence a residual dependence on the
rapidity factorization scale
k_{f}^{+}
is left. The result reads,
for the longitudinally polarized virtual photon:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{virtual}}^{\mathrm{L}}

\displaystyle=x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\frac{2\pi}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}

(316)

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{Tr}\Big\langle\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big[\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V3,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}+\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q2,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q3,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I2,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I3,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I4,sub}}^{0}\Big]+\mathrm{c.c.}\Big\rangle\;.

The explicit expressions for the different contributions are presented
in subsection
9.1.1
. In the transversally
polarized case, the ‘virtual’ cross section is given
by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{virtual}}^{\mathrm{T}}

\displaystyle=x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p})\frac{2\pi}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\frac{1}{2}

(317)

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{Tr}\Big\langle\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\Big[\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle+\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V3}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I3}}^{\lambda}\Big]+\mathrm{c.c.}\Big\rangle\;,

where the expressions for the various terms in are listed in subsection

9.1.2
.

The last term,
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{real}}
, collects those contributions from real radiative corrections that were not yet absorbed into
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{jet}}
or
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{IS}}
, and is given by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\sigma_{\mathrm{real}}=x_{p}f_{q}^{(0)}(x_{p},\mu^{2})\frac{2\pi}{2(p_{1}^{+}+q^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}}\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{1},\vec{q})\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{4\pi p_{3}^{+}}\frac{1}{2}

(318)

\displaystyle\times\!\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}\mathrm{Tr}\Big\langle\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{\eta}\Big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{\eta}\big)+\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{\eta}\Big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle+\!\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{\eta}\big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{\eta}\big)+\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{\eta}\big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle+\!2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{\eta}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}+3}^{\eta}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}+4}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{\eta}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}+4}^{\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}+3}^{\eta}\big)\Big\rangle

and have the same structure irregardless of the photon polarization,
which is why the labels
0
or
\lambda
are omitted. The explicit
expressions of the terms above are listed in subsections (
9.2.1
)
and (
9.2.2
) for the longitudinal resp. transversally
polarized case. Note that, from plus-momentum conservation, we have
that
x_{p}=(p_{1}^{+}+q^{+}+p_{3}^{+})/p_{p}^{+}
.

In the following subsections, we present the results for the different terms in eqs. (
316
), (
317
), and (
318
). It is interesting to remark that, despite the complexity of this calculation, most of the result only depends on two simple sets of color operators, namely:

\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

(319)

The exceptions to this rule are eqs. (
346
), (
349
), (
373
), and (
375
), due to the virtual amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3}}
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q2}}
, which also include a quadrupole:

\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}_{2}}s_{\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}Q_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}_{2}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

(320)

9.1
Virtual contributions

9.1.1
Longitudinal polarization

Self-energy corrections

Diagrams
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}
(
54
)
and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}
(
72
)
can be split up in a finite part, and a part that is divergent for

k^{+}\to 0
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{k^{+}-2p_{0}^{+}}{2(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}+\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg]\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;,

(321)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}\;,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{k^{+}-2p_{1}^{+}}{2(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}+\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg]\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;,

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}+\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}\;.

Making use of the of the following identities (see
Gradshteyn:1943cpj
),
valid for
a\geq c>0
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{c}\mathrm{d}x\,\ln\big(x(a-x)\big)

\displaystyle=-2c+a\ln a-(a-c)\ln(a-c)+c\ln c\;,

(322)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{c}\mathrm{d}x\,x\ln\big(x(a-x)\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Big(-c(a+c)+a^{2}\ln a+c^{2}\ln c+(c^{2}-a^{2})\ln(a-c)\Big)\;,

(with
\mathrm{Li}_{2}
the dilogarithm) the integrals over the gluon
plus-momentum can be evaluated, yielding:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\!\times\!-\frac{3}{4}\Big(-\!i\pi\!+\!2\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\!-\!2\!+\!\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\;,

(323)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0,\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\times-\frac{3}{4}\Big(\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-2\Big)\;.

Hence, we obtain the following virtual contributions to the cross
section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}

(324)

\displaystyle\times-\frac{3}{4}\Big(-i\pi+2\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-2+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\times-\frac{3}{4}\Big(\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}-2\Big)\;.

(325)

We will show below that the contributions
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}
nicely
combine with similar contributions from the vertex corrections. First,
let us write down the contributions from the amplitude
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{0}

(
62
), which reads after subtracting the high-energy
logarithms and multiplying with the LO amplitude:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{0}

(326)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Bigg\{\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1-\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Similarly,
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{0}
(
68
)
leads to the contribution:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{0}

(327)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\Big(-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Bigg\{\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Vertex corrections

The parts from
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}
(
80
)
and
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}
(
98
)
that contain rapidity divergences are:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\;,

(328)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}\;.

When combining them with their counterparts from diagrams
\mathrm{SE1}

and
\mathrm{SE4}
(
321
), and multiplying with
the LO amplitude, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=0\;,

(329)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;.

The plus-momentum integral in the latter expression can be evaluated
explicitly, and yields:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

(330)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg]\;,

where we used the identity (see
Gradshteyn:1943cpj
):

\displaystyle\int_{b}^{c}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{x}\ln\big(x(a-x)\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\ln^{2}c-\frac{1}{2}\ln^{2}b+\ln a\ln\frac{c}{b}-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{c}{a}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{b}{a}\;.

(331)

Two parts are left of
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}

after subtracting the rapidity-divergent part (
328
), namely:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

(332)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}+2p_{1}^{+}}{4p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{4p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle-\frac{M^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{4}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times 2\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{4\pi}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

(333)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\;.

This last contribution still contains a divergence, but it is an unphysical
once. We will show in the next paragraph that it cancels with a similar
spurious pole in
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}
. Also
the next vertex correction
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}
(
86
)
yields both a finite and an unphysical divergent contribution to
the amplitude, and also here, it will cancel with a similar contribution
from
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)

(334)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{e^{i\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}-\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{x}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{z}\Big)}\Big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\Big)\Bigg]\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)

(335)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

The last two vertex-correction amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V3,sub}}^{0}

(
92
) and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}
(
98
)
lead to the following contributions to the cross section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=-g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)\frac{1}{M^{2}}

(336)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\mathbf{\ell}}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{e^{i\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x})}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-\frac{(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\Big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}}{2}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{0}

(337)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg\{-\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}}\Big(1+\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\frac{-1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg[-\frac{q^{+}\big(p_{1}^{+}+2p_{0}^{+}\ln p_{0}^{+}/q^{+}\big)}{4p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2})\ln\frac{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2})}

\displaystyle+\frac{q^{+}}{6p_{0}^{+}}\Big(-3+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\pi^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big(4i\pi-3\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)-6\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{4(k^{+})^{2}\big((k^{+})^{2}+2p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})\big)}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\mathbf{q}^{2}M^{2}\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\;.

Antiquark vertex corrections

As already announced in the previous paragraph,
\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}

(
104
) can be split in both a finite and a divergent
contribution to the cross section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

(338)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\frac{-1}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg\{\frac{M^{2}p_{1}^{+}}{2}\Bigg[\Big(\frac{(7p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{2(p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\Big(-i\mathrm{\pi}+\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+6+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}+4\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big(-\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}+\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+3\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big)\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-2M^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\pi\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}M^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg\}\;.

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

(339)

\displaystyle\times\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)\frac{1}{4}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\;.

Combining eqs. (
339
) and (
333
),
we see that the pathological integrals can be combined and evaluated
with the help of the Sokhotski–Plemelj theorem:

\displaystyle\Bigg[\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}\!+\!\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}\Bigg]\!\Big(\!\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\big)\!\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\big)\!+\!1\!\Big)

(340)

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}-p_{1}^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}-\frac{q^{+}(3p_{0}^{+}-p_{1}^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}+4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}+i0^{+}}

\displaystyle=4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}-i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\Bigg[\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{3}q^{+}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}+\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Bigg]

(341)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)\ln\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{3q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-4p_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}-(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+2\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle-4\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\ln^{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(-\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)+i\pi\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\;.

We end up with the well-behaved result:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}

(342)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{4\pi}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big(\frac{1}{2}+i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)\Big)\Big(i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{3q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-4p_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}-(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-2\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle+4\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}+\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\ln^{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(-\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)+i\pi\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]\;.

Similarly, for amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}
(
110
),
we find the finite part:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

(343)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)\frac{1}{M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times-\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\frac{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}-\hat{M}^{2}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}+\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;,

while the term with the spurious singularity is:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)\frac{1}{M^{2}}

(344)

\displaystyle\times\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

Combining the above with (
335
) and applying (

):

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2,sub}}^{0}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2,sub}}^{0}\Big)\Big|_{\mathrm{spurious}}

(345)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\Big(4\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big(\frac{1}{2}-i\pi+\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;.

The next contribution is due to amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3,sub}}^{0}=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)

(346)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}0\eta^{\prime}}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}2\hat{M}^{2}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}-\mathbf{x}_{2},\hat{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}\Big)}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}_{2}}-\frac{1}{2}Q_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}_{2}}t^{c}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{7p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}+2\frac{p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg\}\;.

Finally:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{0}=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\big)\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}

(347)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{7}{4}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{4p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{2(p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+})}{4q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{(q^{+})^{2}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}}+\frac{3p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}}-\frac{7p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}+(p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+})\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}+3p_{1}^{+}}{2q^{+}}\Big(\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\big)\big(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\!-\!1\big)\!+\!1\Big)\mathbf{q}^{2}\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg]\;.

Instantaneous four-fermion interaction

We obtain the following cross-section contributions, which are all finite:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q1,sub}}^{0}=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{0}\big)

(348)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\Bigg[\Big(2q^{+}-(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\Big(i\pi+\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+2q^{+}+(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})\Big(\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big)\Bigg]\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q2,sub}}^{0}

(349)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}

\displaystyle\times\frac{4\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}\ell_{1}^{+}\frac{1}{(p_{0}^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{4}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{13}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{4}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{\ell_{1}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{4}\Big)^{2}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}\ell_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{4}^{2}}{(q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+})(p_{1}^{+}+\ell_{1}^{+})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{q^{+}-\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}+\frac{\ell_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}\Big)}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}\int_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}_{2}}s_{\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}Q_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}_{2}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle+\Big(2-\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\;,

and finally:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q3,sub}}^{0}=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{0}\big)

(350)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\Bigg[\Big(-2+\frac{p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}}

\displaystyle-2+(2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}-1)\Big(\ln\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big(1+i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\Big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big)+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\big)\Big)\Bigg]\;.

To obtain the expression in the last line of the above result, we
made use of the identity:

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\,\frac{x(1-x)}{(c-x)^{2}}\ln\big(x(1-x)-i0^{+}\big)

(351)

\displaystyle=-2+(2c-1)\Big[\ln\Big(\frac{c-1}{c}\Big)\Big(1+i\pi+\ln c(c-1)\Big)-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(\frac{c-1}{c}\big)+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(\frac{c}{c-1}\big)\Big]

for
c>1
.

interaction

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}N_{c}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\!\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{q^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}}\Big[\big(\!\ln\!\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\!-\!i\pi\big)\Big(\!i\pi\!+\!\ln\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\!+\!p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\!-\!\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(\!-\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\!+\!\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(\!-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)\Big]\;,

and the second:

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\alpha_{s}\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I3,sub}}^{0}
