\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\alpha_{s}\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})}{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{i}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}}\big(1-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)-1\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)-\frac{2q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\;,

and finally:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{0\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I4,sub}}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}N_{c}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(355)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{q^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}}\Big(\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\big)+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\big(-\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\;,

9.1.2
Transverse polarization

Self-energy corrections

The contributions
\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

and
\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

are the same as their counterparts (
324
) and
(
325
) after changing the polarization of the
LO amplitudes which they are proportional to. A similar principle
holds for amplitudes and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}
(
63
)
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}
(
69
), but here
the LO amplitudes cannot be completely factorized out. We, therefore,
present the expressions explicitly:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{\lambda}

(356)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1-\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE3,sub}}^{\lambda}

(357)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{q}})\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathbf{z}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-\mathcal{A}_{0}(\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}})C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}\;.

Vertex corrections

In an attempt to make the expressions below a bit more compact, we
introduce the following notation for the spinor trace over the Dirac
structures, evaluated with the help of identity (
427
)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\Big(\bar{u}_{G}(p_{0}^{+})\gamma^{+}\mathrm{Dirac}^{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{\dagger}u_{G}(p_{1}^{+})\bar{u}_{G}(p_{1}^{+})\gamma^{+}\mathrm{Dirac}_{\mathrm{V}}^{\bar{\eta}\bar{\lambda}\eta^{\prime}}u_{G}(p_{0}^{+})\Big)

(358)

\displaystyle=-8p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\Big[\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Big]\;,

with:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}

\displaystyle\equiv\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-1\big)-1\Big)\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(1-2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)-1\Big)\;,

(359)

\displaystyle\mathcal{\mathcal{O}}_{\mathrm{V}}

\displaystyle\equiv 4\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\;.

We can show that the UV-subtracted vertex corrections
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}

(
76
) and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}

(
99
) contribute as follows to the cross section:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}

(360)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{2\frac{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}+2\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{k^{+}-2p_{1}^{+}-q^{+}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\Big)\Big)\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg\}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}

(361)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{2\frac{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}+2\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{k^{+}-p_{0}^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle+4\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\Bigg[\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)\Bigg]\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\;.

The second lines of the above two expressions contain the rapidity-divergent
parts. These terms nicely combine with the similar parts of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

into completely finite expressions that are independent of the rapidity
factorization scale. Indeed, we have:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\;,

(362)

but also:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;.

(363)

Their sum is:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE1,sub}}^{\lambda}+\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big)\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

(364)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;,

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}

\displaystyle\times\Big[\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big(i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}\Big)-\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-\frac{1}{2}\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big]\;,

in which all dependence on the rapidity cutoff or the rapidity factorization
scale has disappeared. Likewise:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}\;,

(365)

cancels with:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SE2,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}\;,

(366)

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{1/k^{+}}\;.

The completely finite leftovers of (
360
)
and (
361
) are:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V1,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

(367)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{-\frac{p_{1}^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(i\pi+\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}{p_{1}^{+}\big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\big)}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{3p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})+\big((p_{0}^{+})^{2}-(p_{1}^{+})^{2}\big)\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}-p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+2p_{1}^{+})\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}}{(q^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\Big)\Big)\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)\Bigg\}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V4,sub}}^{\lambda}\Big|_{\mathrm{finite}}

(368)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{-\frac{p_{1}^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{q^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}

\displaystyle+4\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\Bigg[\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}q^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}+\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}\big)\Bigg]\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\Delta_{\mathrm{q}},\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\;.

The other contributions to the cross section stemming from vertex
correction diagrams never exhibited divergences to begin with:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V2}}^{\lambda}

(369)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{3}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-1\big)-1\Big)\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(1-2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)-1\Big)\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+4\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;,

and similarly:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\,\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{V3}}^{\lambda}

(370)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{ew}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{q^{+}(k^{+})^{3}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(2\frac{k^{+}-p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}-1\big)-1\Big)\Big(\big(2\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{k^{+}}-1\big)\big(1-2\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{k^{+}}\big)-1\Big)\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+4\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}{k^{+}q^{+}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{q}}}

\displaystyle\times e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

Antiquark vertex corrections

The contribution to the cross section coming from the UV-subtracted
diagram
\mathrm{A1,sub}
is given by the following, quite complicated,
formula:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A1,sub}}^{\lambda}

(371)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO1}}^{\lambda}\big)\times\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\frac{-q^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}+q^{+})+2\big((p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}\big)\ln(p_{0}^{+}/p_{1}^{+})}{2(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(-i\pi+\ln\frac{\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(-\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}+\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+3\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+q^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big(-1+2\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Big(-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\Big)\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\pi\mathcal{B}_{0}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{2p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})-k^{+}q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}\Big(p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big((p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}\big)\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})M^{2}\Big)\Bigg)\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\!\!\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\!\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\!\frac{(k^{+})^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}(2p_{1}^{+}\!-\!k^{+})}\!\Big(\!\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\!-\!\hat{M}^{2}\!-\!\Big(\!\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\!\Big)^{\!2}\Big)\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\!\big(\!\Delta_{\mathrm{P}},\hat{M}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}\!-\!k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\!\big)\!\Bigg\}\;.

The contribution due to the, finite, diagram
\mathrm{A2}
reads:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(372)

\displaystyle\times\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Bigg[\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}iA^{\eta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\eta}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}}\frac{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}\Big)}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\Big)^{2}+\hat{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

Amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{\lambda}
, which did not exhibit
any divergences neither, yields the following term:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A3}}^{\lambda}

(373)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{ew}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\Bigg[\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{V}}\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}iA^{\bar{\lambda}}\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}-\mathbf{x}_{2},\hat{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{12}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}\frac{\mathbf{k}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\mathbf{k}^{2}}\frac{\Big(\mathbf{k}^{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\eta}}\Big)}{\Big(\mathbf{k}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}\Big)^{2}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}(q^{+})^{2}}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}\Big)}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}_{2}}s_{\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}Q_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}_{2}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

Finally, the contribution due to the UV-subtracted amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{\lambda}
is given by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{A4,sub}}^{\lambda}

(374)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO2}}^{\lambda}\big)\times\frac{1}{2}\frac{(q^{+})^{2}}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}C_{F}}{\pi}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\frac{-q^{+}(2p_{0}^{+}+q^{+})+2\big((p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}\big)\ln(p_{0}^{+}/p_{1}^{+})}{2(q^{+})^{2}}\ln\frac{p_{1}^{+}\Delta_{\mathrm{UV}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}{(q^{+})^{2}}\Big(-\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}+\ln^{2}\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+3\ln\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)+\mathrm{Li}_{2}\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+q^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big(-1+2\ln\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\int_{p_{1}^{+}}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\frac{(k^{+})^{2}}{2p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}\Bigg[\Big(\hat{Q}^{2}-\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\Big)^{2}\Big)\mathcal{E}_{V}+\frac{k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\mathcal{O}_{\mathrm{V}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\pi\mathcal{B}_{1}\big(0,\hat{Q}^{2},\frac{p_{0}^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)\Bigg\}\;.

Instantaneous interactions

We conclude this overview of virtual NLO contributions to the cross
section with those coming from instantaneous interactions. For a transversely
polarized photon, only a couple of them are nonzero; for the four-fermion
vertex we have:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{Q2}}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{ew}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

(375)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{q^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{4}{(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})^{2}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+k^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\Big(\frac{2k^{+}}{q^{+}}-1\Big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{3},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}iA^{i}\big(\mathbf{x}_{12},\tilde{M}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1},\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{13}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{23}}}{\Big(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}+\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}+p_{1}^{+}}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\Big)^{2}+\frac{p_{1}^{+}k^{+}p_{0}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}}{(q^{+}-k^{+})(p_{1}^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\int e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\Big(\frac{q^{+}-k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{x}_{1}\Big)}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}_{2}}s_{\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}Q_{\mathbf{x}_{1}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{3}\mathbf{x}_{2}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

The non-vanishing contributions due to the instantaneous
gq\gamma q
vertex read:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I2}}^{\lambda}

(376)

\displaystyle=-g_{\mathrm{ew}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times 4\int_{0}^{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}k^{+}}{k^{+}}\Big(\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})\frac{p_{0}^{+}\big((p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}\big)+p_{1}^{+}k^{+}(k^{+}-p_{0}^{+}-p_{1}^{+})}{k^{+}q^{+}p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}-k^{+})}

\displaystyle\times\!\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\!-\!\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\!+\!C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;,

and:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{LO}}^{\lambda\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{I3}}^{\lambda}

(377)

\displaystyle=-g_{\mathrm{ew}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{p_{1}^{+}}\mathrm{d}k^{+}\frac{2(k^{+}-p_{1}^{+})\Big(q^{+}(k^{+})^{2}-k^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+})^{2}+2p_{1}^{+}\big((p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+})^{2}\big)\Big)}{(p_{1}^{+})^{3}q^{+}(p_{0}^{+}-k^{+})}

\displaystyle\times\!\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{P}}\big)e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}e^{i\frac{k^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\!-\!\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\!+\!C_{F}N_{c}\big)\;.

9.2
Real NLO corrections

9.2.1
Longitudinal polarization

Initial-state radiation

During the analysis of initial-state radiation in section
6
,
we have extracted the part of amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2}}^{0\eta}

that, upon squaring or multiplying with amplitude
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{0\eta}
,
would lead to collinear divergences. The collinear-safe leftovers
yield the following finite contribution to the cross section:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\Big(2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{0\eta}\big)+\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{0\eta}\Big|^{2}\Big)

(378)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}N_{c}C_{F}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-2\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\Bigg]\;.

Diagrams
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3}}^{0\eta}
and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS4}}^{0\eta}

never lead to collinear singularities. Their sum, multiplied with
its complex conjugate, traced and integrated over the gluon transverse
momentum, reads:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{0\eta}\Big|^{2}

(379)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}C_{F}N_{c}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Bigg(\Big(\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{4q^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}+2\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Bigg)\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)

\displaystyle+2\frac{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}\big((p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}\big)}{(p_{0}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}}\mathbf{q}^{2}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4p_{1}^{+}(p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\mathbf{q}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\frac{2(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})(q^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{p_{3}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Note that the integrals that appear in the above formula have analytical
solutions in terms of Macdonald functions, which are easily obtained
using the Schwinger trick together with formula (
403
):

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x},\Delta\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}K_{0}\big(\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}\big)\;,

(380)

\displaystyle\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi}\sqrt{\frac{\mathbf{x}^{2}}{\Delta}}K{}_{1}\big(\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}\big)\;,

\displaystyle\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{i}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot\mathbf{x}}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}\!+\!\Delta)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\!\frac{1}{4\pi}\frac{\mathbf{x}^{i}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}}\!\Big(\!K{}_{1}\big(\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}\big)\!-\!\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}K{}_{0}\big(\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}\big)\!-\!\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}K_{2}\big(\sqrt{\mathbf{x}^{2}\Delta}\big)\!\Big)\,.

Moreover, we have a contribution from the following interference term:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{0\eta}\big)

(381)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}C_{F}N_{c}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z}})}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{j}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{\mathbf{z}})}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{3}^{+}p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)+1\Big)-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{2(p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{p_{3}^{+}}\Bigg)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2}^{j}

\displaystyle-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}\big((p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{3}^{+}+p_{0}^{+})^{2}\big)}{p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{q}^{i}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Final-state radiation

In the analysis of collinear divergences in final-state radiation

7
, we have noted that the amplitudes
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}}^{0\eta}

and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}
exhibit collinear-like behavior
even when the gluon and the quark are not grouped inside the jet.
Even though the jet function cuts off the collinear pole in this ‘outside-jet’
configuration, there are large collinear logarithms that combine with
the ‘inside-jet’ contributions. We have extracted the
part of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2}}^{0\eta}
responsible for these
large logs outside the jet, and its leftover, as well as its interference
with
\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}
, is completely finite.
This results in the following, finite, contribution to the cross section:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\Big(\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{0\eta}\big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}\big)\Big)

(382)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}g_{s}^{2}C_{F}N_{c}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}\frac{e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\frac{\big((p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})\mathbf{q}-q^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\big)^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}-M^{2}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-2\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\frac{\frac{\big((p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})\mathbf{q}-q^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{1}+\mathbf{p}_{3})\big)^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}-M^{2}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\Bigg]\;.

Moreover, we have the following finite contributions to the cross
section stemming from real final-state radiation:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{0\eta}\big|^{2}

(383)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}N_{c}C_{F}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot\mathbf{x}}\Bigg[\Bigg(\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle+2\Big(\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}+\frac{4q^{+}p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg)\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})

\displaystyle+\frac{2(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+})^{2}\big((p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}\big)}{(p_{1}^{+})^{2}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{2(p_{1}^{+})^{2}+(2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})(p_{3}^{+}+q^{+})}{p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})^{2}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg\}\;,

and:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{0\eta}\big)

(384)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}N_{c}C_{F}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}e^{i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})}

\displaystyle\times\!\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{i}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{i}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\!\Bigg[\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}\!-\!p_{3}^{+}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}{\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!-\!\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}\!+\!p_{3}^{+}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}\!+\!\mathbf{p}_{1}\!-\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}\!+\!p_{3}^{+}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}\!+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)+1\Big)-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Bigg)iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})

\displaystyle-p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\frac{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}\Big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Initial-final state radiation interference

Finally, in our calculation, the interference between initial- and
final state radiation never leads to collinear divergences, although
it constitutes an important contribution to the high-energy resummation.
We have:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1}+4}^{0\eta}\big)

(385)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)-\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Bigg)\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\big)^{j}

\displaystyle-p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\frac{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{j}\Big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})\Bigg\}\;.

Moreover:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}+2}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0\eta}\big)

(386)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}e^{-i\mathbf{p}_{3}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\mathbf{p}_{3}^{i}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}^{i}}{\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{i}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{p_{3}^{+}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}+\mathbf{p}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}-p_{3}^{+}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}{\frac{p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}-\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)^{2}+p_{3}^{+}\big(\mathbf{p}_{3}+\mathbf{p}_{1}-\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{3}^{+}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}{(q^{+})^{2}}M^{2}}

Furthermore:

(387)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}-p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})\Bigg\}\;.

The final contribution to the finite real NLO corrections is:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{0\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}}{M^{2}}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle-p_{1}^{+}\frac{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\eta}}\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{2p_{0}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\big)\Bigg]

9.2.2
Transverse polarization

Initial-state radiation

Firstly, we have the finite leftovers of those initial-state diagrams
that constituted the real part of the DGLAP equations:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\Big(2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{\lambda\eta}\big)+\Big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS2,finite}}^{\lambda\eta}\Big|^{2}\Big)

(389)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}N_{c}C_{F}8p_{0}^{+}p_{1}^{+}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}}+1\big)\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{\mathbf{x}}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{(p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{0}^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{(q^{+})^{2}p_{3}^{+}\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}\big)}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{(q^{+})^{2}p_{3}^{+}\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}\big)}{p_{3}^{+}\big(q^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}+p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}\big)^{2}+p_{1}^{+}q^{+}p_{0R}^{+}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}p_{3}^{+}p_{0}^{+}M^{2}}-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)\Bigg]

where we used eq. (
428
). Similarly:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\big|\tilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{\lambda\eta}\big|^{2}

(390)

\displaystyle=\!\int\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\Big)^{2}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z}}e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\!\Bigg[\!\Big(\big(1\!+\!\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}\!+\!1\Big)\!\Big(\big(1\!+\!2\frac{p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}\!+\!1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\!\frac{(q^{+})^{2}\mathbf{q}^{2}}{\big(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!+\!(p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+})M^{2}\big)^{2}}A^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})A^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})

\displaystyle+\!4\Big(\frac{p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})}\Big)^{2}\Big((p_{0}^{+})^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}\Big)\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)

\displaystyle+\!\frac{p_{1}^{+}q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{i}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}\!+\!(p_{1}^{+}\!+\!p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\!\Big(\!iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)\!-\!iA^{i}(\mathbf{x}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}})\mathcal{K}\big(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\!-\!\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{IS}}\big)\!\Big)\!

\displaystyle\times\!\Bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)-1\Big)

\displaystyle+\!\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}-2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Bigg)\Bigg]\;.

Furthermore, we have, adapting identity (

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1+2}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{\lambda\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}e^{i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{z})}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Final-state radiation

Using identity (
428
), what is left after the analysis of collinear final-state divergences can be cast in the following form:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\Big(\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{0\eta}\big|^{2}+2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2,finite}}^{0\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS3}}^{0\eta}\big)\Big)

(392)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{i\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}}{(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}M^{2}}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}p_{1}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}M^{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg]\;.

Likewise, one obtains for the amplitudes that never contributed to the jet function:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\big|\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1+4}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\big|^{2}

(393)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Bigg\{\Big(\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)^{2}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})^{2}}

\displaystyle+4\frac{(p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+})^{2}\big((p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}+(p_{0}^{+})^{2}\big)}{(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}(p_{0}^{+})^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})^{2}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{i}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{i}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)-1\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\Big)\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}-\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-4\int_{k_{\mathrm{min}}^{+}}^{k_{f}^{+}}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\Big(\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}\Big)^{2}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}\Bigg\}\;.

Next, we find the following result for the interference terms, using the trace identity (
427
):

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1+4}}^{\lambda\eta}\big)

(394)

\displaystyle=-g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}C_{F}N_{c}\big(s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}p_{3}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}e^{-i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})}e^{i\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\int_{\mathbf{z}}\int_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\eta^{\prime}}e^{-i\boldsymbol{\ell}\cdot(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{0}^{+}}\frac{q^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\lambda^{\prime}}+\frac{p_{0}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{\big(\boldsymbol{\ell}+\frac{p_{3}^{+}}{p_{1}^{+}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\big)^{2}+\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}}}+\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{q}^{\lambda^{\prime}}}{p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathbf{q}^{2}+(p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})M^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\Bigg(\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)+1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)+1\Big)\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}

\displaystyle-4\frac{(p_{0}^{+}+p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+})^{2}}{p_{3}^{+}q^{+}}\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\delta^{\eta^{\prime}\lambda^{\prime}}p_{0\scriptscriptstyle{R}}^{+}\mathcal{K}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})\Bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{+}}\Big)\Big(\big(1+2\frac{p_{1}^{+}+p_{3}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)-1\Big)

initial- and final state radiation in the transversely polarized case:

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS1+2}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS1+4}}^{\lambda\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\frac{q^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{\bar{\lambda}}}{p_{0}^{+}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}^{2}+p_{1}^{+}M^{2}}iA^{\bar{\eta}}(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z},\Delta_{\mathrm{FS}})

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\Big(\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)^{2}+1\Big)\delta^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\delta^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}-4\big(1+\frac{2p_{1}^{+}}{q^{+}}\big)\big(1+\frac{2p_{0}^{+}}{p_{3}^{+}}\big)\epsilon^{\bar{\eta}\eta^{\prime}}\epsilon^{\bar{\lambda}\lambda^{\prime}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{PS}(\vec{p}_{3})\mathrm{Tr}\,2\mathrm{Re}\big(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IS3+4}}^{\lambda\eta\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{FS2+3}}^{\lambda\eta}\big)

(398)

\displaystyle=-g_{\mathrm{em}}^{2}\alpha_{s}8p_{1}^{+}p_{0}^{+}2\mathrm{Re}\int_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}}e^{i\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\cdot(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime})}\big(\frac{N_{c}^{2}}{2}s_{\mathbf{z}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{z}}-\frac{1}{2}s_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}+C_{F}N_{c}\big)
